========================================
FILE: App.jsx
========================================
import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route } from "react-router-dom";

import { ThemeProvider } from "./context/ThemeContext";
import { AuthProvider } from "./context/AuthContext";

import { USER_ROLES } from "./config/constants";

import Layout from "./components/Layout";
import ProtectedRoute from "./components/ProtectedRoute";

import Home from "./pages/Home";
import Login from "./pages/Login";
import Dashboard from "./pages/Dashboard";
import Documents from "./pages/Documents";
import Recovery from "./pages/Recovery";
import Audit from "./pages/Audit";
import Unauthorized from "./pages/Unauthorized";

function App() {
  return (
    <ThemeProvider>
      <AuthProvider>
        <BrowserRouter>
          <Routes>
            <Route element={<Layout />}>
              {/* Public pages */}
              <Route path="/" element={<Home />} />
              <Route path="/login" element={<Login />} />
              <Route path="/unauthorized" element={<Unauthorized />} />

              {/* All authenticated users */}
              <Route element={<ProtectedRoute />}>
                <Route path="/dashboard" element={<Dashboard />} />

                <Route path="/documents" element={<Documents />} />
              </Route>

              {/* Admin and Operator */}
              <Route
                element={
                  <ProtectedRoute
                    allowedRoles={[USER_ROLES.ADMIN, USER_ROLES.OPERATOR]}
                  />
                }
              >
                <Route path="/recovery" element={<Recovery />} />
              </Route>

              {/* Admin and Auditor */}
              <Route
                element={
                  <ProtectedRoute
                    allowedRoles={[USER_ROLES.ADMIN, USER_ROLES.AUDITOR]}
                  />
                }
              >
                <Route path="/audit" element={<Audit />} />
              </Route>
            </Route>
          </Routes>
        </BrowserRouter>
      </AuthProvider>
    </ThemeProvider>
  );
}

export default App;

  ShieldCheck,
  LogIn,
  LogOut,
  UserCircle,
} from "lucide-react";

import { useTheme } from "../context/ThemeContext";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";

import { NAVIGATION_ITEMS } from "../data/navigation";

import "../styles/navbar.css";

function Navbar() {
  const { isDark, toggleTheme } = useTheme();

  const { user, isAuthenticated, logout, hasRole } = useAuth();

  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const visibleNavigation = NAVIGATION_ITEMS.filter((item) => {
    if (item.public) {
      return true;
    }

    if (!isAuthenticated) {
      return false;
    }

    if (!item.roles) {
      return true;
    }

    return hasRole(item.roles);
  });

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();

    navigate("/", { replace: true });
  };

  return (
    <header className="navbar">
      <Link to="/" className="navbar-brand">
        <div className="brand-icon">
          <ShieldCheck size={23} />
        </div>

        <div className="brand-details">
          <span className="brand-name">EvidenceVault</span>

          <span className="brand-subtitle">SECURE EVIDENCE SYSTEM</span>
        </div>
      </Link>

      <nav className="navbar-links">
        {visibleNavigation.map((item) => {
          const Icon = item.icon;

          return (
            <NavLink
              key={item.path}
              to={item.path}
              end={item.path === "/"}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                `nav-link ${isActive ? "active" : ""}`
              }
            >
              <Icon size={17} />
              <span>{item.label}</span>
            </NavLink>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      <div className="navbar-actions">
        <button
          className="theme-toggle"
          onClick={toggleTheme}
          title={isDark ? "Switch to light mode" : "Switch to dark mode"}
          aria-label="Toggle theme"
        >
          {isDark ? <Sun size={19} /> : <Moon size={19} />}
        </button>

        {isAuthenticated ? (
          <div className="user-session">
            <div className="user-info">
              <UserCircle size={18} />

              <div className="user-details">
                <span>{user.username}</span>
                <small>{user.roles?.join(", ")}</small>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              className="logout-button"
              onClick={handleLogout}
              title="Logout"
            >
              <LogOut size={17} />
              <span>Logout</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <Link to="/login" className="login-button">
            <LogIn size={17} />
            <span>Sign In</span>
          </Link>
        )}
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

export default Navbar;

========================================
FILE: components\ProtectedRoute.jsx

import { Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";

import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";

function ProtectedRoute({ allowedRoles = [] }) {
  const { isAuthenticated, hasRole } = useAuth();

  const location = useLocation();

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace state={{ from: location }} />;
  }

  if (allowedRoles.length > 0 && !hasRole(allowedRoles)) {
    return <Navigate to="/unauthorized" replace />;
  }

  return <Outlet />;
}

export default ProtectedRoute;

function StatusBadge({ status }) {
  const normalizedStatus = String(status || "UNKNOWN").toUpperCase();

  const isHealthy = [
    "UP",
    "SUCCESS",
    "COMPLETED",
    "HEALTHY",
    "RUNNING",
  ].includes(normalizedStatus);

  const isWarning = ["NOT_CHECKED", "PENDING", "IN_PROGRESS"].includes(
    normalizedStatus,
  );

  const color = isHealthy ? "success" : isWarning ? "warning" : "danger";

  return (
    <span className={`status-badge ${color}`}>
      <span className="status-indicator"></span>
      {normalizedStatus.replaceAll("_", " ")}
    </span>
  );
}

export default StatusBadge;

import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from "react";

import {
  loginUser,
  saveAuthSession,
  getStoredUser,
  clearAuthSession,
} from "../services/authService";

const AuthContext = createContext(null);

export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(() => getStoredUser());

  const [isAuthenticated, setIsAuthenticated] = useState(() =>
    Boolean(localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-token") && getStoredUser()),
  );

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  // Restore the saved session on application startup.
  useEffect(() => {
    const storedUser = getStoredUser();
    const storedToken = localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-token");

    if (storedUser && storedToken) {
      setUser(storedUser);
      setIsAuthenticated(true);
    } else {
      clearAuthSession();
      setUser(null);
      setIsAuthenticated(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const login = async (credentials) => {
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const authData = await loginUser(credentials);

      if (!authData?.accessToken) {
        throw new Error("Authentication token was not received.");
      }

      saveAuthSession(authData);

      setUser({
        username: authData.username,
        roles: authData.roles || [],
        tokenType: authData.tokenType,
        expiresIn: authData.expiresIn,
      });

      setIsAuthenticated(true);

      return authData;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const logout = () => {
    clearAuthSession();

    setUser(null);
    setIsAuthenticated(false);
  };

  const hasRole = (allowedRoles = []) => {
    if (!user || !Array.isArray(user.roles)) {
      return false;
    }

    return allowedRoles.some((role) => user.roles.includes(role));
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        user,
        isAuthenticated,
        loading,
        login,
        logout,
        hasRole,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth() {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useAuth must be used inside AuthProvider");
  }

  return context;
}

========================================
FILE: context\ThemeContext.jsx
========================================
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from "react";

const ThemeContext = createContext(null);

export function ThemeProvider({ children }) {
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState(() => {
    return localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-theme") || "dark";
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", theme);

    localStorage.setItem("evidence-vault-theme", theme);
  }, [theme]);

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    setTheme((currentTheme) => (currentTheme === "dark" ? "light" : "dark"));
  };

  return (
    <ThemeContext.Provider
      value={{
        theme,
        toggleTheme,
        isDark: theme === "dark",
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </ThemeContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useTheme() {
  const context = useContext(ThemeContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useTheme must be used inside ThemeProvider");
  }

  return context;
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

function StatCard({ title, value, subtitle, icon: Icon, tone = "blue" }) {
  return (
    <article className="dashboard-stat-card">
      <div className={`stat-icon stat-icon-${tone}`}>
        <Icon size={21} />
      </div>

      <div className="stat-content">
        <p>{title}</p>
        <h2>{value}</h2>
        <span>{subtitle}</span>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

function StatusBadge({ status }) {
  const normalized = String(status || "UNKNOWN").toUpperCase();

  let className = "status-neutral";

  if (["SUCCESS", "COMPLETED", "UP", "VERIFIED"].includes(normalized)) {
    className = "status-success";
  } else if (["FAILED", "DOWN", "ERROR", "MISMATCH"].includes(normalized)) {
    className = "status-danger";
  } else if (["RECOVERY_REQUIRED", "PENDING", "WARNING"].includes(normalized)) {
    className = "status-warning";
  }

  return (
    <span className={`status-badge ${className}`}>
      {normalized.replaceAll("_", " ")}
    </span>
  );
}

function EmptyState({ message }) {
  return <div className="dashboard-empty">{message}</div>;
}

export default function Dashboard() {
  const [dashboard, setDashboard] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [pageError, setPageError] = useState("");

  const loadDashboard = useCallback(async (isRefresh = false) => {
    if (isRefresh) {
      setRefreshing(true);
    } else {
      setLoading(true);
    }

    setPageError("");

    try {
      const result = await getDashboardData();
      setDashboard(result);
    } catch (error) {
      setPageError(error?.message || "Unable to load dashboard data.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      setRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadDashboard();
  }, [loadDashboard]);

  const metrics = useMemo(
    () => normalizeArray(dashboard?.metrics),
    [dashboard],
  );

  const drills = useMemo(() => normalizeArray(dashboard?.drills), [dashboard]);

  const manifests = useMemo(
    () => normalizeArray(dashboard?.manifests),
    [dashboard],
  );

  const drillSummary = useMemo(() => calculateDrillSummary(drills), [drills]);

  const metricSummary = useMemo(
    () => calculateMetricSummary(metrics),
    [metrics],
  );

  const latestDrill = useMemo(() => getLatestRecord(drills), [drills]);

  const latestManifest = useMemo(() => getLatestRecord(manifests), [manifests]);

  const documentCount = dashboard?.documents?.totalElements ?? 0;
  const userCount = dashboard?.system?.totalUsers ?? "N/A";

  const rtoChartData = useMemo(() => {
    return [...metrics]
      .filter(
        (item) =>
          item.recovery_rto_seconds !== null &&
          item.recovery_rto_seconds !== undefined,
      )
      .sort((a, b) => new Date(a.created_at) - new Date(b.created_at))
      .slice(-8)
      .map((item, index) => ({
        name: formatDate(item.created_at),
        rto: Number(item.recovery_rto_seconds),
        label: `Drill ${index + 1}`,
      }));
  }, [metrics]);

  const drillChartData = [
    { name: "Successful", value: drillSummary.successful },
    { name: "Failed", value: drillSummary.failed },
    { name: "Attention required", value: drillSummary.attentionRequired },
  ].filter((item) => item.value > 0);

  const latestFailureType =
    latestDrill?.failure_type?.replaceAll("_", " ") || "No drill recorded";

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="dashboard-loading">
        <RefreshCw className="loading-spin" size={25} />
        <p>Loading your dashboard...</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (pageError && !dashboard) {
    return (
      <div className="dashboard-error">
        <AlertTriangle size={30} />
        <h2>Dashboard could not be loaded</h2>
        <p>{pageError}</p>
        <button onClick={() => loadDashboard()}>
          <RefreshCw size={16} /> Try again
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const system = dashboard?.system;

  return (
    <main className="dashboard-page">
      {/* Dashboard heading */}
      <section className="dashboard-heading">
        <div>
          <span className="dashboard-eyebrow">DOCUMENT EVIDENCE VAULT</span>

          <h1>Dashboard</h1>

          <p>
            Monitor document protection, regional verification and disaster
            recovery performance.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          className="refresh-button"
          onClick={() => loadDashboard(true)}
          disabled={refreshing}
        >
          <RefreshCw size={17} className={refreshing ? "loading-spin" : ""} />
          {refreshing ? "Refreshing..." : "Refresh"}
        </button>
      </section>

      {pageError && (
        <div className="dashboard-warning">
          <AlertTriangle size={17} />
          Some dashboard information could not be refreshed. Check the
          individual sections below.
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Infrastructure health */}
      <section className="dashboard-section">
        <div className="section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Infrastructure overview</h2>
            <p>Application and database status</p>
          </div>
          <Activity size={20} />
        </div>

        <div className="infrastructure-grid">
          <div className="infrastructure-card">
            <div className="infrastructure-icon">
              <Cloud size={22} />
            </div>

            <div>
              <span>Spring Boot application</span>
              <StatusBadge status={system?.application || "UNKNOWN"} />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="infrastructure-card">
            <div className="infrastructure-icon">
              <Database size={22} />
            </div>

            <div>
              <span>MySQL database</span>
              <StatusBadge status={system?.database || "UNKNOWN"} />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="infrastructure-card">
            <div className="infrastructure-icon">
              <Users size={22} />
            </div>

            <div>
              <span>Registered users</span>
              <strong>{userCount}</strong>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="infrastructure-card">
            <div className="infrastructure-icon">
              <FileText size={22} />
            </div>

            <div>
              <span>Stored document records</span>
              <strong>{documentCount}</strong>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {system?.message && <p className="system-message">{system.message}</p>}

        {dashboard?.errors?.system && (
          <p className="section-error">
            Infrastructure status unavailable: {dashboard.errors.system}
          </p>
        )}
      </section>

      {/* Main KPI cards */}
      <section className="dashboard-section">
        <div className="section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Disaster recovery performance</h2>
            <p>Measurements from recorded recovery operations</p>
          </div>
          <ShieldCheck size={20} />
        </div>

        <div className="dashboard-stats-grid">

            title="Total DR drills"
            value={drillSummary.total}
            subtitle="Recorded drill operations"

            tone="blue"

                    ))}
                  </Pie>
                  <Tooltip />
                  <Legend />
                </PieChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            ) : (
              <EmptyState message="No drill outcomes available yet." />
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Latest manifest */}
      <section className="dashboard-section">
        <div className="section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Latest recovery evidence</h2>
            <p>Most recently recorded recovery manifest</p>
          </div>
          <FileCheck2 size={20} />
        </div>

        {latestManifest ? (
          <div className="manifest-card">
            <div className="manifest-icon">
              <FileCheck2 size={24} />
            </div>

            <div className="manifest-details">
              <h3>{latestManifest.manifest_id || "Recovery manifest"}</h3>
              <p>
                Document ID: {latestManifest.document_id}
                {" Â· "}
                {latestManifest.failure_type?.replaceAll("_", " ")}
              </p>
              <span>Created: {formatDate(latestManifest.created_at)}</span>
            </div>

            <div className="manifest-status">
              <StatusBadge status={latestManifest.verification_status} />
              <strong>{formatSeconds(latestManifest.rto_seconds)}</strong>
              <span>Recorded RTO</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <EmptyState message="No recovery manifests have been recorded." />
        )}
      </section>

      {/* Recent activity */}
      <section className="dashboard-section">
        <div className="section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Recent disaster recovery activity</h2>
            <p>Latest recorded drill operations</p>
          </div>
          <Activity size={20} />
        </div>

        {drills.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="table-wrapper">
            <table className="dashboard-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Document</th>
                  <th>Failure type</th>
                  <th>Primary</th>
                  <th>Secondary</th>
                  <th>Status</th>
                  <th>Date</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>

              <tbody>
                {drills.slice(0, 8).map((drill, index) => (
                  <tr key={drill.id ?? index}>
                    <td>#{drill.document_id}</td>

                    <td>{drill.failure_type?.replaceAll("_", " ") || "N/A"}</td>

                    <td>
                      <StatusBadge
                        status={drill.primary_verified ? "VERIFIED" : "FAILED"}
                      />
                    </td>

                    <td>
                      <StatusBadge
                        status={
                          drill.secondary_verified ? "VERIFIED" : "FAILED"
                        }
                      />
                    </td>

                    <td>
                      <StatusBadge status={drill.drill_status} />
                    </td>

                    <td>{formatDate(drill.created_at)}</td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <EmptyState message="No disaster recovery activity is available." />
        )}

        {dashboard?.errors?.drills && (
          <p className="section-error">
            Drill history could not be retrieved. Your account may not have
            permission to view this information.
          </p>
        )}
      </section>

      {/* Quick actions */}
      <section className="dashboard-quick-actions">
        <div>
          <h2>Manage your documents</h2>
          <p>Continue with document upload and management.</p>
        </div>

        <Link to="/documents" className="primary-action">
          Open document management
          <ArrowUpRight size={17} />
        </Link>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

========================================
FILE: pages\Documents.jsx

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

import {
  Search,
  Upload,
  Download,
  Eye,
  Trash2,
  RotateCcw,
  FileText,
  FileImage,
  File,
  RefreshCw,
  X,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  FolderOpen,
  AlertCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
} from "lucide-react";

import {
  getDocuments,
  uploadDocument,
  deleteDocument,
  restoreDocument,
  downloadDocument,
} from "../services/documentService";

import {
  formatFileSize,
  formatDocumentDate,
  getFileExtension,
  isPreviewable,
  getDocumentStatus,
} from "../services/documentUtils";

import "../styles/documents.css";

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

function DocumentIcon({ contentType }) {
  if (contentType?.startsWith("image/")) {
    return <FileImage size={20} />;
  }

  if (contentType === "application/pdf") {
    return <FileText size={20} />;
  }

  return <File size={20} />;
}

function DocumentStatus({ status }) {
  const value = getDocumentStatus(status);

  const className =
    value === "ACTIVE" || value === "AVAILABLE"
      ? "document-status-active"
      : value === "DELETED"
        ? "document-status-deleted"
        : "document-status-neutral";

  return (
    <span className={`document-status ${className}`}>
      {value.replaceAll("_", " ")}
    </span>
  );
}

function UploadModal({ onClose, onUpload, uploading }) {
  const [file, setFile] = useState(null);
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState("");
  const [validationError, setValidationError] = useState("");

  function handleSubmit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();

    if (!file) {
      setValidationError("Please select a document.");
      return;
    }

    if (!category.trim()) {
      setValidationError("Please enter a document category.");
      return;
    }

    setValidationError("");
    onUpload(file, category, description);
  }

  return (
    <div className="document-modal-overlay" onClick={onClose}>
      <section
        className="document-modal"
        onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <div className="modal-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Upload document</h2>
            <p>Add a new document to your evidence vault.</p>
          </div>

          <button className="icon-button" onClick={onClose} type="button">
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <label className="document-form-label">Select document</label>

          <label className="file-drop-area">
            <Upload size={28} />

            <strong>{file ? file.name : "Choose a file to upload"}</strong>

            <span>
              {file
                ? formatFileSize(file.size)
                : "Select a file from your computer"}
            </span>

            <input
              type="file"
              onChange={(event) => setFile(event.target.files?.[0] || null)}
            />
          </label>

          <label className="document-form-label">
            Category <span>*</span>
          </label>

          <input
            className="document-input"
            type="text"
            placeholder="e.g. Legal, Financial, Reports"
            value={category}
            onChange={(event) => setCategory(event.target.value)}
            required
          />

          <label className="document-form-label">Description</label>

          <textarea
            className="document-input document-textarea"
            placeholder="Enter a short description..."
            value={description}
            onChange={(event) => setDescription(event.target.value)}
            rows={3}
          />

          {validationError && (
            <p className="document-validation-error">{validationError}</p>
          )}

          <div className="modal-actions">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="secondary-button"
              onClick={onClose}
              disabled={uploading}
            >
              Cancel
            </button>

            <button
              type="submit"
              className="document-primary-button"
              disabled={uploading}
            >
              <Upload size={16} />
              {uploading ? "Uploading..." : "Upload document"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

function PreviewModal({ document, previewUrl, onClose }) {
  return (
    <div className="document-modal-overlay" onClick={onClose}>
      <section
        className="document-preview-modal"
        onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <div className="modal-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>{document.originalFilename}</h2>
            <p>Document preview</p>
          </div>

          <button className="icon-button" onClick={onClose}>
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {document.contentType?.startsWith("image/") ? (
          <div className="image-preview-area">
            <img src={previewUrl} alt={document.originalFilename} />
          </div>
        ) : (
          <iframe
            className="pdf-preview-frame"
            src={previewUrl}
            title={document.originalFilename}
          />
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Documents() {
  const [documents, setDocuments] = useState([]);
  const [page, setPage] = useState(0);
  const [totalPages, setTotalPages] = useState(0);
  const [totalElements, setTotalElements] = useState(0);

  const [keyword, setKeyword] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [actionId, setActionId] = useState(null);

  const [showUpload, setShowUpload] = useState(false);
  const [previewDocument, setPreviewDocument] = useState(null);
  const [previewUrl, setPreviewUrl] = useState("");

  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const loadDocuments = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const result = await getDocuments({
        keyword,
        category,
        status,
        page,
        size: PAGE_SIZE,
      });

      setDocuments(Array.isArray(result?.content) ? result.content : []);
      setTotalPages(result?.totalPages ?? 0);
      setTotalElements(result?.totalElements ?? 0);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || "Unable to load documents.");
      setDocuments([]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [keyword, category, status, page]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadDocuments();
  }, [loadDocuments]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (previewUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(previewUrl);
    };
  }, [previewUrl]);

  const activeDocuments = useMemo(
    () =>
      documents.filter((item) => getDocumentStatus(item.status) !== "DELETED"),
    [documents],
  );

  async function handleUpload(file, selectedCategory, description) {
    setUploading(true);
    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    try {
      await uploadDocument(file, selectedCategory, description);

      setShowUpload(false);
      setPage(0);
      setKeyword("");
      setCategory("");
      setStatus("");

      setMessage("Document uploaded successfully.");
      await loadDocuments();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || "Document upload failed.");
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleDownload(document) {
    setActionId(document.id);
    setError("");

    try {
      const blob = await downloadDocument(document.id);
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

      const link = window.document.createElement("a");
      link.href = url;
      link.download = document.originalFilename || "document";
      window.document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      link.remove();
      setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || "Download failed.");
    } finally {
      setActionId(null);
    }
  }

  async function handlePreview(document) {
    if (!isPreviewable(document.contentType, document.originalFilename)) {
      setError("Preview is available only for PDFs and supported images.");
      return;
    }

    setActionId(document.id);
    setError("");

    try {
      const blob = await downloadDocument(document.id);
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

      setPreviewUrl(url);
      setPreviewDocument(document);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || "Unable to preview document.");
    } finally {
      setActionId(null);
    }
  }

  function closePreview() {
    setPreviewDocument(null);
    setPreviewUrl("");
  }

  async function handleDelete(document) {
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      `Are you sure you want to delete "${document.originalFilename}"?`,
    );

    if (!confirmed) return;

    setActionId(document.id);
    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    try {
      await deleteDocument(document.id);
      setMessage("Document deleted successfully.");
      await loadDocuments();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || "Unable to delete document.");
    } finally {
      setActionId(null);
    }
  }

  async function handleRestore(document) {
    setActionId(document.id);
    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    try {
      await restoreDocument(document.id);
      setMessage("Document restored successfully.");
      await loadDocuments();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || "Unable to restore document.");
    } finally {
      setActionId(null);
    }
  }

  function applySearch(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setPage(0);
    loadDocuments();
  }

  function clearFilters() {
    setKeyword("");
    setCategory("");
    setStatus("");
    setPage(0);
  }

  return (
    <main className="documents-page">
      <section className="documents-heading">
        <div>
          <span className="documents-eyebrow">EVIDENCE VAULT</span>

          <h1>Document Management</h1>

          <p>Upload, organize, preview and manage your protected documents.</p>
        </div>

        <button
          className="document-primary-button"
          onClick={() => setShowUpload(true)}
        >
          <Upload size={17} />
          Upload document
        </button>
      </section>

      {/* Summary */}
      <section className="document-summary-grid">
        <div className="document-summary-card">
          <div className="document-summary-icon">
            <FolderOpen size={21} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span>Total records</span>
            <strong>{totalElements}</strong>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="document-summary-card">
          <div className="document-summary-icon summary-green">
            <CheckCircle2 size={21} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span>Active on this page</span>
            <strong>{activeDocuments.length}</strong>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="document-summary-card">
          <div className="document-summary-icon summary-purple">
            <FileText size={21} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span>Current page</span>
            <strong>
              {totalPages ? page + 1 : 0} / {totalPages}
            </strong>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Alerts */}
      {message && (
        <div className="document-alert document-alert-success">
          <CheckCircle2 size={17} />
          {message}
          <button onClick={() => setMessage("")}>
            <X size={15} />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="document-alert document-alert-error">
          <AlertCircle size={17} />
          {error}
          <button onClick={() => setError("")}>
            <X size={15} />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Search and filters */}
      <section className="document-toolbar">
        <form className="document-search-form" onSubmit={applySearch}>
          <div className="document-search-box">
            <Search size={18} />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Search document name..."
              value={keyword}
              onChange={(event) => setKeyword(event.target.value)}
            />
          </div>

          <input
            className="document-filter-input"
            type="text"
            placeholder="Category"
            value={category}
            onChange={(event) => setCategory(event.target.value)}
          />

          <select
            className="document-filter-input"
            value={status}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setStatus(event.target.value);
              setPage(0);
            }}
          >
            <option value="">All statuses</option>
            <option value="ACTIVE">Active</option>
            <option value="DELETED">Deleted</option>
          </select>

          <button type="submit" className="document-search-button">
            Search
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            className="document-clear-button"
            onClick={clearFilters}
          >
            Clear
          </button>
        </form>

        <button
          className="document-refresh-button"
          onClick={loadDocuments}
          disabled={loading}
          title="Refresh documents"
        >
          <RefreshCw size={17} className={loading ? "documents-spin" : ""} />
        </button>
      </section>

      {/* Document table */}
      <section className="documents-table-card">
        <div className="documents-table-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Your documents</h2>
            <p>
              Showing {documents.length} of {totalElements} records
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="documents-empty-state">
            <RefreshCw className="documents-spin" size={25} />
            <p>Loading documents...</p>
          </div>
        ) : documents.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="documents-empty-state">
            <FolderOpen size={35} />
            <h3>No documents found</h3>
            <p>Try changing your search filters or upload a new document.</p>
            <button
              className="document-primary-button"
              onClick={() => setShowUpload(true)}
            >
              <Upload size={16} />
              Upload document
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="documents-table-scroll">
            <table className="documents-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Document</th>
                  <th>Category</th>
                  <th>Size</th>
                  <th>Checksum</th>
                  <th>Status</th>
                  <th>Uploaded by</th>
                  <th>Upload date</th>
                  <th>Actions</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>

              <tbody>
                {documents.map((document) => {
                  const deleted =
                    getDocumentStatus(document.status) === "DELETED";

                  return (
                    <tr key={document.id}>
                      <td>
                        <div className="document-name-cell">
                          <div className="document-file-icon">
                            <DocumentIcon contentType={document.contentType} />
                          </div>

                          <div>
                            <strong>{document.originalFilename}</strong>
                            <span>
                              {getFileExtension(document.originalFilename)}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      <td>{document.category || "N/A"}</td>

                      <td>{formatFileSize(document.fileSizeBytes)}</td>

                      <td>
                        <span
                          className="checksum-value"
                          title={document.sha256Checksum}
                        >
                          {document.sha256Checksum
                            ? `${document.sha256Checksum.slice(0, 12)}...`
                            : "N/A"}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      <td>
                        <DocumentStatus status={document.status} />
                      </td>

                      <td>{document.uploadedBy || "N/A"}</td>

                      <td>{formatDocumentDate(document.createdAt)}</td>

                      <td>
                        <div className="document-row-actions">
                          {!deleted && (
                            <>
                              <button
                                title="Preview"
                                disabled={
                                  actionId === document.id ||
                                  !isPreviewable(
                                    document.contentType,
                                    document.originalFilename,
                                  )
                                }
                                onClick={() => handlePreview(document)}
                              >
                                <Eye size={16} />
                              </button>

                              <button
                                title="Download"
                                disabled={actionId === document.id}
                                onClick={() => handleDownload(document)}
                              >
                                <Download size={16} />
                              </button>

                              <button
                                title="Delete"
                                className="delete-action"
                                disabled={actionId === document.id}
                                onClick={() => handleDelete(document)}
                              >
                                <Trash2 size={16} />
                              </button>
                            </>
                          )}

                          {deleted && (
                            <button
                              title="Restore"
                              className="restore-action"
                              disabled={actionId === document.id}
                              onClick={() => handleRestore(document)}
                            >
                              <RotateCcw size={16} />
                            </button>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Pagination */}
        <div className="documents-pagination">
          <span>
            Page {totalPages ? page + 1 : 0} of {totalPages}
          </span>

          <div>
            <button
              disabled={page <= 0 || loading}
              onClick={() => setPage((current) => current - 1)}
            >
              <ChevronLeft size={17} />
              Previous
            </button>

            <button
              disabled={page >= totalPages - 1 || loading}
              onClick={() => setPage((current) => current + 1)}
            >
              Next
              <ChevronRight size={17} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {showUpload && (
        <UploadModal
          onClose={() => setShowUpload(false)}
          onUpload={handleUpload}
          uploading={uploading}
        />
      )}

      {previewDocument && previewUrl && (
        <PreviewModal
          document={previewDocument}
          previewUrl={previewUrl}
          onClose={closePreview}
        />
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";

import {
  ShieldCheck,
  User,
  Lock,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  ArrowRight,
  AlertCircle,
} from "lucide-react";

import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";

import "../styles/login.css";

function Login() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();

  const { login, loading } = useAuth();

  const [credentials, setCredentials] = useState({
    username: "",
    password: "",
  });

  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const handleChange = (event) => {
    const { name, value } = event.target;

    setCredentials((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      [name]: value,
    }));
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");

    if (!credentials.username.trim() || !credentials.password) {
      setError("Please enter both username and password.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      await login(credentials);

      const destination = location.state?.from?.pathname || "/dashboard";

      navigate(destination, { replace: true });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Login failed. Please try again.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="login-page">
      <div className="login-decoration"></div>

      <div className="login-container">
        <div className="login-brand-icon">
          <ShieldCheck size={38} />
        </div>

        <div className="login-heading">
          <h1>Welcome Back</h1>

          <p>Sign in to access your secure evidence vault.</p>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="login-error">
            <AlertCircle size={17} />
            <span>{error}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <div className="form-group">
            <label htmlFor="username">Username</label>

            <div className="input-wrapper">
              <User size={18} />

              <input
                id="username"
                name="username"
                type="text"
                placeholder="Enter your username"
                value={credentials.username}
                onChange={handleChange}
                autoComplete="username"
                required
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="form-group">
            <label htmlFor="password">Password</label>

            <div className="input-wrapper">
              <Lock size={18} />

              <input
                id="password"
                name="password"
                type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                placeholder="Enter your password"
                value={credentials.password}
                onChange={handleChange}
                autoComplete="current-password"
                required
              />

              <button
                type="button"
                className="password-toggle"
                onClick={() => setShowPassword((previous) => !previous)}
                aria-label={showPassword ? "Hide password" : "Show password"}
              >
                {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={18} /> : <Eye size={18} />}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button type="submit" className="login-submit" disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? "Signing In..." : "Sign In"}

            {!loading && <ArrowRight size={18} />}
          </button>
        </form>

        <div className="login-security">
          <ShieldCheck size={15} />

          <span>Secure authentication powered by JWT</span>
        </div>

        <Link to="/" className="back-home">
          Back to Home
        </Link>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default Login;

========================================
FILE: services\api.js

import { APP_CONFIG } from "../config/constants";

const API_BASE_URL = APP_CONFIG.API_BASE_URL;

export async function apiRequest(endpoint, options = {}) {
  const token = localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-token");

  const headers = {
    ...options.headers,
  };

  // Add JSON content type when sending JSON.
  // Do not set it for FormData uploads.
  if (!(options.body instanceof FormData) && options.body !== undefined) {
    headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json";
  }

  // Attach JWT when available.
  if (token) {
    headers["Authorization"] = `Bearer ${token}`;
  }

  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${endpoint}`, {
    ...options,
    headers,
  });

  if (response.status === 204) {
    return null;
  }

  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") || "";

  const responseData = contentType.includes("application/json")
    ? await response.json()
    : await response.text();

  if (!response.ok) {
    const message =
      typeof responseData === "object"
        ? responseData?.message || responseData?.error
        : responseData;

    throw new Error(message || `Request failed: ${response.status}`);
  }

  return responseData;
}

import { apiRequest } from "./api";

export async function loginUser(credentials) {
  const response = await apiRequest("/v1/auth/login", {
    method: "POST",
    body: JSON.stringify({
      username: credentials.username,
      password: credentials.password,
    }),
  });

  return response;
}

export function saveAuthSession(authData) {
  localStorage.setItem("evidence-vault-token", authData.accessToken);

  localStorage.setItem(
    "evidence-vault-user",
    JSON.stringify({
      username: authData.username,
      roles: authData.roles,
      tokenType: authData.tokenType,
      expiresIn: authData.expiresIn,
    }),
  );
}

export function getStoredUser() {
  const storedUser = localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-user");

  if (!storedUser) {
    return null;
  }

  try {
    return JSON.parse(storedUser);
  } catch {
    clearAuthSession();
    return null;
  }
}

export function clearAuthSession() {
  localStorage.removeItem("evidence-vault-token");
  localStorage.removeItem("evidence-vault-user");
}

import { apiRequest } from "./api";

export async function getSystemStatus() {
  return apiRequest("/v1/system/status");
}

export async function getRecoveryMetrics() {
  return apiRequest("/v1/dr/metrics");
}

export async function getDrillHistory() {
  return apiRequest("/v1/dr/drills");
}

export async function getRecoveryManifests() {
  return apiRequest("/v1/dr/manifests");
}

export async function getDocumentSummary() {
  return apiRequest("/v1/documents?page=0&size=1");
}

export async function getDashboardData() {
  const requests = await Promise.allSettled([
    getSystemStatus(),
    getRecoveryMetrics(),
    getDrillHistory(),
    getRecoveryManifests(),
    getDocumentSummary(),
  ]);

  const getValue = (index, fallback) =>
    requests[index].status === "fulfilled" ? requests[index].value : fallback;

  const getError = (index) =>
    requests[index].status === "rejected"
      ? requests[index].reason?.message || "Request failed"
      : null;

  return {
    system: getValue(0, null),
    metrics: getValue(1, []),
    drills: getValue(2, []),
    manifests: getValue(3, []),
    documents: getValue(4, null),

    errors: {
      system: getError(0),
      metrics: getError(1),
      drills: getError(2),
      manifests: getError(3),
      documents: getError(4),
    },
  };
}

FILE: services\dashboardUtils.js

export function normalizeArray(data) {
  return Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
}
export function average(values) {
  const valid = values
    .filter((value) => value !== null && value !== undefined && value !== "")
    .map(Number)
    .filter(Number.isFinite);

  if (!valid.length) return null;

  return Number(
    (valid.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / valid.length).toFixed(3),
  );
}

export function calculateDrillSummary(drills) {
  const total = drills.length;

  const successful = drills.filter(
    (item) => item.drill_status === "SUCCESS",
  ).length;

  const failed = drills.filter((item) => item.drill_status === "FAILED").length;

  const attentionRequired = drills.filter(
    (item) => item.drill_status === "RECOVERY_REQUIRED",
  ).length;

  const recoveryOperations = drills.filter(
    (item) => item.recovery_performed === true,
  ).length;

  const corruptionDrills = drills.filter(
    (item) =>
      item.corruption_detected === true ||
      item.failure_type === "SIMULATED_CORRUPTION",
  ).length;

  const primaryFailures = drills.filter(
    (item) => item.failure_type === "SIMULATED_PRIMARY_FAILURE",
  ).length;

  const checksumMismatches = drills.filter((item) => {
    const primaryMismatch =
      item.primary_checksum &&
      item.expected_checksum &&
      item.primary_checksum !== item.expected_checksum;

    const secondaryMismatch =
      item.secondary_checksum &&
      item.expected_checksum &&
      item.secondary_checksum !== item.expected_checksum;

    return primaryMismatch || secondaryMismatch;
  }).length;

  return {
    total,
    successful,
    failed,
    attentionRequired,
    recoveryOperations,
    corruptionDrills,
    primaryFailures,
    checksumMismatches,

    successRate: total ? Number(((successful / total) * 100).toFixed(1)) : null,
  };
}

export function calculateMetricSummary(metrics) {
  const rtoValues = metrics.map(
    (item) => item.recovery_rto_seconds ?? item.recovery_workflow_seconds,
  );

  const rpoValues = metrics
    .map((item) => item.estimated_rpo_seconds)
    .filter((value) => value !== null && value !== undefined);

  const replicationCompleted = metrics.filter(
    (item) => item.replication_status === "COMPLETED",
  ).length;

  const replicationFailed = metrics.filter(
    (item) => item.replication_status === "FAILED",
  ).length;

  const replicationTimedOut = metrics.filter(
    (item) => item.replication_timed_out === true,
  ).length;

  return {
    averageRto: average(rtoValues),
    averageRpo: average(rpoValues),
    replicationCompleted,
    replicationFailed,
    replicationTimedOut,
  };
}

export function getLatestRecord(records) {
  if (!records.length) return null;

  return [...records].sort(
    (a, b) => new Date(b.created_at) - new Date(a.created_at),
  )[0];
}

export function formatSeconds(value) {
  if (
    value === null ||
    value === undefined ||
    !Number.isFinite(Number(value))
  ) {
    return "N/A";
  }

  return `${Number(value).toFixed(3)} sec`;
}

export function formatPercentage(value) {
  return value === null || value === undefined ? "N/A" : `${value}%`;
}

export function formatDate(value) {
  if (!value) return "N/A";

  const date = new Date(value);

  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? "N/A" : date.toLocaleString();
}

FILE: services\documentFileService.js

import { APP_CONFIG } from "../config/constants";

const API_BASE_URL = APP_CONFIG.API_BASE_URL;

function getFriendlyError(status) {
  switch (status) {
    case 401:
      return "Your session has expired. Please sign in again.";
    case 403:
      return "You don't have permission to access this document.";
    case 404:
      return "The document could not be found.";
    case 500:
      return "The server couldn't retrieve this document. Please try again.";
    default:
      return "Unable to retrieve the document. Please try again.";
  }
}

export async function fetchDocumentBlob(documentId) {
  const token = localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-token");

  if (!token) {
    throw new Error("Your session has expired. Please sign in again.");
  }

  const response = await fetch(
    `${API_BASE_URL}/v1/documents/${documentId}/download`,
    {
      method: "GET",
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      },
    },
  );

  if (!response.ok) {
    let message = getFriendlyError(response.status);

    try {
      const errorData = await response.json();

      // Don't expose raw server exceptions to the UI.
      if (response.status >= 500) {
        message =
          "The server couldn't retrieve this document. Please try again.";
      } else if (response.status === 401 || response.status === 403) {
        message = getFriendlyError(response.status);
      } else if (errorData.message) {
        message = errorData.message;
      }
    } catch {
      // Keep the friendly fallback message.
    }

    throw new Error(message);
  }

  return await response.blob();
}

export function downloadBlob(blob, filename) {
  const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

  const link = document.createElement("a");
  link.href = objectUrl;
  link.download = filename || "document";
  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  link.remove();

  URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl);
}

FILE: services\documentService.js

import { apiRequest } from "./api";

const DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT = "/v1/documents";

export async function getDocuments({
  keyword = "",
  category = "",
  status = "",
  page = 0,
  size = 10,
} = {}) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({
    page: String(page),
    size: String(size),
  });

  if (keyword.trim()) params.set("keyword", keyword.trim());
  if (category.trim()) params.set("category", category.trim());
  if (status.trim()) params.set("status", status.trim());

  return apiRequest(`${DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT}?${params.toString()}`);
}

export async function uploadDocument(file, category, description) {
  const formData = new FormData();

  formData.append("file", file);
  formData.append("category", category);

  if (description.trim()) {
    formData.append("description", description.trim());
  }

  return apiRequest(`${DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT}/upload`, {
    method: "POST",
    body: formData,
  });
}

export async function deleteDocument(documentId) {
  return apiRequest(`${DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT}/${documentId}`, {
    method: "DELETE",
  });
}

export async function restoreDocument(documentId) {
  return apiRequest(`${DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT}/${documentId}/restore`, {
    method: "POST",
  });
}

// Downloads the file as a Blob because the existing apiRequest helper
// handles JSON/text responses rather than binary file responses.
export async function downloadDocument(documentId) {
  const token = localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-token");

  if (!token) {
    throw new Error("Your session has expired. Please sign in again.");
  }

  const response = await fetch(
    `/api${DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT}/${documentId}/download`,
    {
      method: "GET",
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      },
    },
  );

  if (!response.ok) {
    const messages = {
      401: "Your session has expired. Please sign in again.",
      403: "You don't have permission to access this document.",
      404: "The requested document was not found.",
      500: "The server couldn't retrieve this document. Please try again.",
    };

    throw new Error(
      messages[response.status] ||
        "Unable to download the document. Please try again.",
    );
  }

  return response.blob();
}

FILE: services\documentUtils.js

export function formatFileSize(bytes) {
  if (bytes === null || bytes === undefined) return "N/A";

  const size = Number(bytes);

  if (!Number.isFinite(size) || size < 0) return "N/A";
  if (size === 0) return "0 Bytes";

  const units = ["Bytes", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"];
  const index = Math.min(
    Math.floor(Math.log(size) / Math.log(1024)),
    units.length - 1,
  );

  return `${(size / Math.pow(1024, index)).toFixed(2)} ${units[index]}`;
}

export function formatDocumentDate(value) {
  if (!value) return "N/A";

  const date = new Date(value);

  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? "N/A" : date.toLocaleString();
}

export function getFileExtension(filename = "") {
  return filename.includes(".")
    ? filename.split(".").pop().toUpperCase()
    : "FILE";
}

export function isPreviewable(contentType = "", filename = "") {
  const type = contentType.toLowerCase();
  const extension = filename.split(".").pop().toLowerCase();

  const supportedTypes = [
    "application/pdf",
    "image/png",
    "image/jpeg",
    "image/jpg",
    "image/webp",
    "image/gif",
  ];

  const supportedExtensions = ["pdf", "png", "jpg", "jpeg", "webp", "gif"];

  return (
    supportedTypes.includes(type) ||
    type.startsWith("image/") ||
    supportedExtensions.includes(extension)
  );
}

export function getDocumentStatus(status) {
  return String(status || "UNKNOWN").toUpperCase();
}

========================================
FILE: App.jsx
========================================
import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route } from "react-router-dom";

import { ThemeProvider } from "./context/ThemeContext";
import { AuthProvider } from "./context/AuthContext";

import { USER_ROLES } from "./config/constants";

import Layout from "./components/Layout";
import ProtectedRoute from "./components/ProtectedRoute";

import Home from "./pages/Home";
import Login from "./pages/Login";
import Dashboard from "./pages/Dashboard";
import Documents from "./pages/Documents";
import Recovery from "./pages/Recovery";
import Audit from "./pages/Audit";
import Unauthorized from "./pages/Unauthorized";

function App() {
  return (
    <ThemeProvider>
      <AuthProvider>
        <BrowserRouter>
          <Routes>
            <Route element={<Layout />}>
              {/* Public pages */}
              <Route path="/" element={<Home />} />
              <Route path="/login" element={<Login />} />
              <Route path="/unauthorized" element={<Unauthorized />} />

              {/* All authenticated users */}
              <Route element={<ProtectedRoute />}>
                <Route path="/dashboard" element={<Dashboard />} />

                <Route path="/documents" element={<Documents />} />
              </Route>

              {/* Admin and Operator */}
              <Route
                element={
                  <ProtectedRoute
                    allowedRoles={[USER_ROLES.ADMIN, USER_ROLES.OPERATOR]}
                  />
                }
              >
                <Route path="/recovery" element={<Recovery />} />
              </Route>

              {/* Admin and Auditor */}
              <Route
                element={
                  <ProtectedRoute
                    allowedRoles={[USER_ROLES.ADMIN, USER_ROLES.AUDITOR]}
                  />
                }
              >
                <Route path="/audit" element={<Audit />} />
              </Route>
            </Route>
          </Routes>
        </BrowserRouter>
      </AuthProvider>
    </ThemeProvider>
  );
}

export default App;

import { NavLink, Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

import {
  Sun,
  Moon,
  ShieldCheck,
  LogIn,
  LogOut,
  UserCircle,
} from "lucide-react";

import { useTheme } from "../context/ThemeContext";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";

import { NAVIGATION_ITEMS } from "../data/navigation";

import "../styles/navbar.css";

function Navbar() {
  const { isDark, toggleTheme } = useTheme();

  const { user, isAuthenticated, logout, hasRole } = useAuth();

  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const visibleNavigation = NAVIGATION_ITEMS.filter((item) => {
    if (item.public) {
      return true;
    }

    if (!isAuthenticated) {
      return false;
    }

    if (!item.roles) {
      return true;
    }

    return hasRole(item.roles);
  });

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();

    navigate("/", { replace: true });
  };

  return (
    <header className="navbar">
      <Link to="/" className="navbar-brand">
        <div className="brand-icon">
          <ShieldCheck size={23} />
        </div>

        <div className="brand-details">
          <span className="brand-name">EvidenceVault</span>

          <span className="brand-subtitle">SECURE EVIDENCE SYSTEM</span>
        </div>
      </Link>

      <nav className="navbar-links">
        {visibleNavigation.map((item) => {
          const Icon = item.icon;

          return (
            <NavLink
              key={item.path}
              to={item.path}
              end={item.path === "/"}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                `nav-link ${isActive ? "active" : ""}`
              }
            >
              <Icon size={17} />
              <span>{item.label}</span>
            </NavLink>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      <div className="navbar-actions">
        <button
          className="theme-toggle"
          onClick={toggleTheme}
          title={isDark ? "Switch to light mode" : "Switch to dark mode"}
          aria-label="Toggle theme"
        >
          {isDark ? <Sun size={19} /> : <Moon size={19} />}
        </button>

        {isAuthenticated ? (
          <div className="user-session">
            <div className="user-info">
              <UserCircle size={18} />

              <div className="user-details">
                <span>{user.username}</span>
                <small>{user.roles?.join(", ")}</small>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              className="logout-button"
              onClick={handleLogout}
              title="Logout"
            >
              <LogOut size={17} />
              <span>Logout</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <Link to="/login" className="login-button">
            <LogIn size={17} />
            <span>Sign In</span>
          </Link>
        )}
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

export default Navbar;

========================================
FILE: components\ProtectedRoute.jsx
========================================
import { Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";

import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";

function ProtectedRoute({ allowedRoles = [] }) {
  const { isAuthenticated, hasRole } = useAuth();

  const location = useLocation();

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace state={{ from: location }} />;
  }

  if (allowedRoles.length > 0 && !hasRole(allowedRoles)) {
    return <Navigate to="/unauthorized" replace />;
  }

  return <Outlet />;
}

export default ProtectedRoute;

function StatusBadge({ status }) {
  const normalizedStatus = String(status || "UNKNOWN").toUpperCase();

  const isHealthy = [
    "UP",
    "SUCCESS",
    "COMPLETED",
    "HEALTHY",
    "RUNNING",
  ].includes(normalizedStatus);

  const isWarning = ["NOT_CHECKED", "PENDING", "IN_PROGRESS"].includes(
    normalizedStatus,
  );

  const color = isHealthy ? "success" : isWarning ? "warning" : "danger";

  return (
    <span className={`status-badge ${color}`}>
      <span className="status-indicator"></span>
      {normalizedStatus.replaceAll("_", " ")}
    </span>
  );
}

export default StatusBadge;

FILE: context\AuthContext.jsx

import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from "react";

import {
  loginUser,
  saveAuthSession,
  getStoredUser,
  clearAuthSession,
} from "../services/authService";

const AuthContext = createContext(null);

export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(() => getStoredUser());

  const [isAuthenticated, setIsAuthenticated] = useState(() =>
    Boolean(localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-token") && getStoredUser()),
  );

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  // Restore the saved session on application startup.
  useEffect(() => {
    const storedUser = getStoredUser();
    const storedToken = localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-token");

    if (storedUser && storedToken) {
      setUser(storedUser);
      setIsAuthenticated(true);
    } else {
      clearAuthSession();
      setUser(null);
      setIsAuthenticated(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const login = async (credentials) => {
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const authData = await loginUser(credentials);

      if (!authData?.accessToken) {
        throw new Error("Authentication token was not received.");
      }

      saveAuthSession(authData);

      setUser({
        username: authData.username,
        roles: authData.roles || [],
        tokenType: authData.tokenType,
        expiresIn: authData.expiresIn,
      });

      setIsAuthenticated(true);

      return authData;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const logout = () => {
    clearAuthSession();

    setUser(null);
    setIsAuthenticated(false);
  };

  const hasRole = (allowedRoles = []) => {
    if (!user || !Array.isArray(user.roles)) {
      return false;
    }

    return allowedRoles.some((role) => user.roles.includes(role));
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        user,
        isAuthenticated,
        loading,
        login,
        logout,
        hasRole,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth() {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useAuth must be used inside AuthProvider");
  }

  return context;
}

========================================
FILE: context\ThemeContext.jsx
========================================
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from "react";

const ThemeContext = createContext(null);

export function ThemeProvider({ children }) {
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState(() => {
    return localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-theme") || "dark";
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", theme);

    localStorage.setItem("evidence-vault-theme", theme);
  }, [theme]);

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    setTheme((currentTheme) => (currentTheme === "dark" ? "light" : "dark"));
  };

  return (
    <ThemeContext.Provider
      value={{
        theme,
        toggleTheme,
        isDark: theme === "dark",
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </ThemeContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useTheme() {
  const context = useContext(ThemeContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useTheme must be used inside ThemeProvider");
  }

  return context;
}
