function Audit() {
  const [documents, setDocuments] = useState([]);
  const [selectedDocumentId, setSelectedDocumentId] = useState("");
  const [auditLogs, setAuditLogs] = useState([]);

  const [loadingDocuments, setLoadingDocuments] = useState(true);
  const [loadingAudit, setLoadingAudit] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState("");

  const loadDocuments = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoadingDocuments(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const response = await getDocuments({
        page: 0,
        size: 100,
      });

      const records = response?.content || [];
      setDocuments(records);

      if (records.length > 0) {
        setSelectedDocumentId(
          (currentId) => currentId || String(records[0].id),
        );
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Unable to load documents.");
    } finally {
      setLoadingDocuments(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const loadAuditHistory = useCallback(async (documentId) => {
    if (!documentId) {
      setAuditLogs([]);
      return;
    }

    setLoadingAudit(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const response = await getDocumentAuditHistory(documentId);
      setAuditLogs(Array.isArray(response) ? response : []);
    } catch (err) {
      setAuditLogs([]);
      setError(err.message || "Unable to load audit history.");
    } finally {
      setLoadingAudit(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadDocuments();
  }, [loadDocuments]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedDocumentId) {
      loadAuditHistory(selectedDocumentId);
    }
  }, [selectedDocumentId, loadAuditHistory]);

  const filteredLogs = useMemo(() => {
    const term = searchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!term) return auditLogs;

    return auditLogs.filter((log) =>
      [log.username, log.action, log.details, log.documentId]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" ")
        .toLowerCase()
        .includes(term),
    );
  }, [auditLogs, searchTerm]);

  const selectedDocument = documents.find(
    (document) => String(document.id) === selectedDocumentId,
  );

  const formatDate = (value) => {
    if (!value) return "Unknown date";

    const date = new Date(value);

    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return value;

    return date.toLocaleString();
  };

  const formatAction = (action) =>
    String(action || "UNKNOWN").replaceAll("_", " ");

  const getActionClass = (action) => {
    const value = String(action || "").toUpperCase();

    if (value.includes("DELETE")) return "audit-action-delete";
    if (value.includes("RESTORE")) return "audit-action-restore";
    if (value.includes("UPLOAD")) return "audit-action-upload";
    if (value.includes("DOWNLOAD")) return "audit-action-download";
    if (value.includes("PREVIEW")) return "audit-action-preview";

    return "audit-action-default";
  };

  return (
    <main className="audit-page">
      <section className="audit-header">
        <div>
          <div className="audit-title-row">
            <ShieldCheck size={28} />
            <h1>Audit Trail</h1>
          </div>

          <p>Track document activities, users, and recorded events.</p>
        </div>

        <button
          className="audit-refresh-button"
          onClick={() => {
            if (selectedDocumentId) {
              loadAuditHistory(selectedDocumentId);
            } else {
              loadDocuments();
            }
          }}
          disabled={loadingAudit || loadingDocuments}
        >
          <RefreshCw size={16} />
          Refresh
        </button>
      </section>

      <section className="audit-controls">
        <div className="audit-document-selector">
          <label htmlFor="audit-document">Select Document</label>

          <select
            id="audit-document"
            value={selectedDocumentId}
            onChange={(event) => setSelectedDocumentId(event.target.value)}
            disabled={loadingDocuments || documents.length === 0}
          >
            {documents.length === 0 && (
              <option value="">No documents available</option>
            )}

            {documents.map((document) => (
              <option key={document.id} value={String(document.id)}>
                #{document.id} â€” {document.originalFilename}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        <div className="audit-search">
          <Search size={17} />

          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Search user, action or details..."
            value={searchTerm}
            onChange={(event) => setSearchTerm(event.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
      </section>

      {selectedDocument && (
        <section className="audit-document-card">
          <div className="audit-document-icon">
            <FileText size={22} />
          </div>

          <div>
            <h3>{selectedDocument.originalFilename}</h3>
            <p>
              Document ID: #{selectedDocument.id}
              {" Â· "}
              Category: {selectedDocument.category}
              {" Â· "}
              Status: {selectedDocument.status}
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="audit-error">
          <AlertCircle size={18} />
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <section className="audit-summary">
        <div className="audit-summary-icon">
          <Activity size={21} />
        </div>

        <div>
          <span>Total Recorded Events</span>
          <strong>{auditLogs.length}</strong>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="audit-history-section">
        <div className="audit-section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Activity History</h2>
            <p>Events are displayed in newest-first order.</p>
          </div>

          <span className="audit-event-count">
            {filteredLogs.length} events
          </span>
        </div>

        {loadingDocuments || loadingAudit ? (
          <div className="audit-state">
            <RefreshCw className="audit-spin" size={24} />
            <p>Loading audit history...</p>
          </div>
        ) : filteredLogs.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="audit-state">
            <FileText size={32} />
            <h3>No audit events found</h3>
            <p>
              {auditLogs.length === 0
                ? "No recorded activities are available for this document."
                : "Try a different search term."}
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="audit-table-wrapper">
            <table className="audit-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Event ID</th>
                  <th>Action</th>
                  <th>User</th>
                  <th>Details</th>
                  <th>Timestamp</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>

              <tbody>
                {filteredLogs.map((log) => (
                  <tr key={log.id}>
                    <td>#{log.id}</td>

                    <td>
                      <span
                        className={`audit-action ${getActionClass(log.action)}`}
                      >
                        {formatAction(log.action)}
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    <td>
                      <span className="audit-user">
                        <User size={15} />
                        {log.username}
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    <td>{log.details || "â€”"}</td>

                    <td>
                      <span className="audit-time">
                        <Clock size={14} />
                        {formatDate(log.createdAt)}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

export default Audit;

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";

  AlertTriangle,

  ArrowUpRight,

  Cloud,
  Database,
  FileCheck2,
  FileText,

  ShieldCheck,
  Users,
  XCircle,
} from "lucide-react";

import {
  Bar,
  BarChart,
  CartesianGrid,
  Cell,
  Legend,
  Pie,
  PieChart,
  ResponsiveContainer,
  Tooltip,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
} from "recharts";

import { getDashboardData } from "../services/dashboardService";

import {
  normalizeArray,
  calculateDrillSummary,
  calculateMetricSummary,
  getLatestRecord,
  formatSeconds,
  formatDate,
} from "../services/dashboardUtils";

import "../styles/dashboard.css";

function StatCard({ title, value, subtitle, icon: Icon, tone = "blue" }) {
  return (
    <article className="dashboard-stat-card">
      <div className={`stat-icon stat-icon-${tone}`}>
        <Icon size={21} />
      </div>

      <div className="stat-content">
        <p>{title}</p>
        <h2>{value}</h2>
        <span>{subtitle}</span>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

function StatusBadge({ status }) {
  const normalized = String(status || "UNKNOWN").toUpperCase();

  let className = "status-neutral";

  if (["SUCCESS", "COMPLETED", "UP", "VERIFIED"].includes(normalized)) {
    className = "status-success";
  } else if (["FAILED", "DOWN", "ERROR", "MISMATCH"].includes(normalized)) {
    className = "status-danger";
  } else if (["RECOVERY_REQUIRED", "PENDING", "WARNING"].includes(normalized)) {
    className = "status-warning";
  }

  return (
    <span className={`status-badge ${className}`}>
      {normalized.replaceAll("_", " ")}
    </span>
  );
}

function EmptyState({ message }) {
  return <div className="dashboard-empty">{message}</div>;
}

export default function Dashboard() {
  const [dashboard, setDashboard] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [pageError, setPageError] = useState("");

  const loadDashboard = useCallback(async (isRefresh = false) => {
    if (isRefresh) {
      setRefreshing(true);
    } else {
      setLoading(true);
    }

    setPageError("");

    try {
      const result = await getDashboardData();
      setDashboard(result);
    } catch (error) {
      setPageError(error?.message || "Unable to load dashboard data.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      setRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadDashboard();
  }, [loadDashboard]);

  const metrics = useMemo(
    () => normalizeArray(dashboard?.metrics),
    [dashboard],
  );

  const drills = useMemo(() => normalizeArray(dashboard?.drills), [dashboard]);

  const manifests = useMemo(
    () => normalizeArray(dashboard?.manifests),
    [dashboard],
  );

  const drillSummary = useMemo(() => calculateDrillSummary(drills), [drills]);

  const metricSummary = useMemo(
    () => calculateMetricSummary(metrics),
    [metrics],
  );

  const latestDrill = useMemo(() => getLatestRecord(drills), [drills]);

  const latestManifest = useMemo(() => getLatestRecord(manifests), [manifests]);

  const documentCount = dashboard?.documents?.totalElements ?? 0;
  const userCount = dashboard?.system?.totalUsers ?? "N/A";

  const rtoChartData = useMemo(() => {
    return [...metrics]
      .filter(
        (item) =>
          item.recovery_rto_seconds !== null &&
          item.recovery_rto_seconds !== undefined,
      )
      .sort((a, b) => new Date(a.created_at) - new Date(b.created_at))
      .slice(-8)
      .map((item, index) => ({
        name: formatDate(item.created_at),
        rto: Number(item.recovery_rto_seconds),
        label: `Drill ${index + 1}`,
      }));
  }, [metrics]);

  const drillChartData = [
    { name: "Successful", value: drillSummary.successful },
    { name: "Failed", value: drillSummary.failed },
    { name: "Attention required", value: drillSummary.attentionRequired },
  ].filter((item) => item.value > 0);

  const latestFailureType =
    latestDrill?.failure_type?.replaceAll("_", " ") || "No drill recorded";

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="dashboard-loading">
        <RefreshCw className="loading-spin" size={25} />
        <p>Loading your dashboard...</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (pageError && !dashboard) {
    return (
      <div className="dashboard-error">
        <AlertTriangle size={30} />
        <h2>Dashboard could not be loaded</h2>
        <p>{pageError}</p>
        <button onClick={() => loadDashboard()}>
          <RefreshCw size={16} /> Try again
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const system = dashboard?.system;

  return (
    <main className="dashboard-page">
      {/* Dashboard heading */}
      <section className="dashboard-heading">
        <div>
          <span className="dashboard-eyebrow">DOCUMENT EVIDENCE VAULT</span>

          <h1>Dashboard</h1>

          <p>
            Monitor document protection, regional verification and disaster
            recovery performance.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          className="refresh-button"
          onClick={() => loadDashboard(true)}
          disabled={refreshing}
        >
          <RefreshCw size={17} className={refreshing ? "loading-spin" : ""} />
          {refreshing ? "Refreshing..." : "Refresh"}
        </button>
      </section>

      {pageError && (
        <div className="dashboard-warning">
          <AlertTriangle size={17} />
          Some dashboard information could not be refreshed. Check the
          individual sections below.
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Infrastructure health */}
      <section className="dashboard-section">
        <div className="section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Infrastructure overview</h2>
            <p>Application and database status</p>
          </div>
          <Activity size={20} />
        </div>

        <div className="infrastructure-grid">
          <div className="infrastructure-card">
            <div className="infrastructure-icon">
              <Cloud size={22} />
            </div>

            <div>
              <span>Spring Boot application</span>
              <StatusBadge status={system?.application || "UNKNOWN"} />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="infrastructure-card">
            <div className="infrastructure-icon">
              <Database size={22} />
            </div>

            <div>
              <span>MySQL database</span>
              <StatusBadge status={system?.database || "UNKNOWN"} />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="infrastructure-card">
            <div className="infrastructure-icon">
              <Users size={22} />
            </div>

            <div>
              <span>Registered users</span>
              <strong>{userCount}</strong>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="infrastructure-card">
            <div className="infrastructure-icon">
              <FileText size={22} />
            </div>

            <div>
              <span>Stored document records</span>
              <strong>{documentCount}</strong>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {system?.message && <p className="system-message">{system.message}</p>}

        {dashboard?.errors?.system && (
          <p className="section-error">
            Infrastructure status unavailable: {dashboard.errors.system}
          </p>
        )}
      </section>

      {/* Main KPI cards */}
      <section className="dashboard-section">
        <div className="section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Disaster recovery performance</h2>
            <p>Measurements from recorded recovery operations</p>
          </div>
          <ShieldCheck size={20} />
        </div>

        <div className="dashboard-stats-grid">
          <StatCard
            title="Total DR drills"
            value={drillSummary.total}
            subtitle="Recorded drill operations"
            icon={Activity}
            tone="blue"
          />

          <StatCard
            title="Drill success rate"
            value={
              drillSummary.successRate === null
                ? "N/A"
                : `${drillSummary.successRate}%`
            }
            subtitle={`${drillSummary.successful} successful drills`}
            icon={CheckCircle2}
            tone="green"
          />

          <StatCard
            title="Average recovery time"
            value={formatSeconds(metricSummary.averageRto)}
            subtitle="Average recorded RTO"
            icon={Clock3}
            tone="purple"
          />

          <StatCard
            title="Average estimated RPO"
            value={formatSeconds(metricSummary.averageRpo)}
            subtitle="Timestamp-based estimate"
            icon={ArrowDownToLine}
            tone="orange"
          />

          <StatCard
            title="Recovery operations"
            value={drillSummary.recoveryOperations}
            subtitle="Drills where recovery was performed"
            icon={RefreshCw}
            tone="blue"
          />

          <StatCard
            title="Recovery manifests"
            value={manifests.length}
            subtitle="Recorded recovery evidence"
            icon={FileCheck2}
            tone="green"
          />
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Regional verification */}
      <section className="dashboard-section">
        <div className="section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Regional verification</h2>
            <p>
              Results from the most recently recorded DR drill. These are not
              live S3 health checks.
            </p>
          </div>

        </div>

        <div className="region-grid">
          <div className="region-card">
            <div className="region-top">
              <div className="region-icon">IN</div>
              <div>
                <h3>Primary region</h3>
                <span>Mumbai Â· ap-south-1</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="region-result">
              <span>Last recorded verification</span>
              <StatusBadge
                status={
                  latestDrill?.primary_verified === true
                    ? "VERIFIED"
                    : latestDrill?.primary_verified === false
                      ? "FAILED"
                      : "UNKNOWN"
                }
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="region-connection">
            <div className="connection-line"></div>
            <span>Cross-region replication</span>
            <div className="connection-line"></div>
          </div>

          <div className="region-card">
            <div className="region-top">
              <div className="region-icon region-icon-secondary">SG</div>
              <div>
                <h3>Secondary region</h3>
                <span>Singapore Â· ap-southeast-1</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="region-result">
              <span>Last recorded verification</span>
              <StatusBadge
                status={
                  latestDrill?.secondary_verified === true
                    ? "VERIFIED"
                    : latestDrill?.secondary_verified === false
                      ? "FAILED"
                      : "UNKNOWN"
                }
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <p className="region-footnote">
          Latest drill: {formatDate(latestDrill?.created_at)}
        </p>

        {dashboard?.errors?.drills && (
          <p className="section-error">

          </p>
        )}
      </section>

      {/* Replication and integrity */}
      <section className="dashboard-section">
        <div className="section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Replication and integrity</h2>
            <p>Recorded replication and checksum results</p>
          </div>
          <ShieldCheck size={20} />
        </div>

        <div className="analysis-grid">
          <div className="analysis-card">
            <h3>Replication summary</h3>

            <div className="analysis-row">
              <span>Completed</span>
              <strong className="text-success">

              </strong>

              <span>Failed</span>
              <strong className="text-danger">

              </strong>

              <span>Timed out</span>
              <strong>{metricSummary.replicationTimedOut}</strong>
            </div>

            <div className="analysis-row">
              <span>Corruption drills</span>
              <strong>{drillSummary.corruptionDrills}</strong>
            </div>

            <div className="analysis-row">
              <span>Primary failure drills</span>
              <strong>{drillSummary.primaryFailures}</strong>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="analysis-card">
            <h3>Integrity summary</h3>

            <div className="integrity-highlight">
              {drillSummary.checksumMismatches === 0 ? (
                <CheckCircle2 size={27} />
              ) : (
                <AlertTriangle size={27} />
              )}

              <div>
                <strong>{drillSummary.checksumMismatches}</strong>
                <span>Recorded checksum mismatches</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <p>
              Mismatches are counted from drill records where expected and
              actual checksums are available.
            </p>

            <div className="analysis-row">
              <span>Drills requiring attention</span>
              <strong>{drillSummary.attentionRequired}</strong>
            </div>

            <div className="analysis-row">
              <span>Failed drills</span>
              <strong>{drillSummary.failed}</strong>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Charts */}
      <section className="dashboard-section">
        <div className="section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Recovery analytics</h2>
            <p>Visual summary of recorded drill data</p>
          </div>
          <ArrowUpRight size={20} />
        </div>

        <div className="charts-grid">
          <div className="chart-card">
            <div className="chart-heading">
              <h3>Recovery time trend</h3>
              <span>RTO in seconds</span>
            </div>

            {rtoChartData.length > 0 ? (
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>
                <BarChart data={rtoChartData}>
                  <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" vertical={false} />
                  <XAxis dataKey="label" tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
                  <YAxis tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} width={45} />
                  <Tooltip
                    formatter={(value) => [
                      `${Number(value).toFixed(3)} sec`,
                      "RTO",
                    ]}
                  />
                  <Bar
                    dataKey="rto"
                    name="Recovery time"
                    fill="#6478f5"
                    radius={[6, 6, 0, 0]}
                  />
                </BarChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            ) : (
              <EmptyState message="No recovery time measurements available yet." />
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="chart-card">
            <div className="chart-heading">
              <h3>Drill outcomes</h3>
              <span>Recorded drill statuses</span>
            </div>

            {drillChartData.length > 0 ? (
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>
                <PieChart>
                  <Pie
                    data={drillChartData}
                    dataKey="value"
                    nameKey="name"
                    cx="50%"
                    cy="45%"
                    outerRadius={85}
                    label
                  >
                    {drillChartData.map((entry, index) => (
                      <Cell
                        key={entry.name}
                        fill={["#16a36a", "#e05252", "#e4a33b"][index % 3]}
                      />
                    ))}
                  </Pie>
                  <Tooltip />
                  <Legend />
                </PieChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            ) : (
              <EmptyState message="No drill outcomes available yet." />
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Latest manifest */}
      <section className="dashboard-section">
        <div className="section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Latest recovery evidence</h2>
            <p>Most recently recorded recovery manifest</p>
          </div>
          <FileCheck2 size={20} />
        </div>

        {latestManifest ? (
          <div className="manifest-card">
            <div className="manifest-icon">
              <FileCheck2 size={24} />
            </div>

            <div className="manifest-details">
              <h3>{latestManifest.manifest_id || "Recovery manifest"}</h3>
              <p>
                Document ID: {latestManifest.document_id}
                {" Â· "}
                {latestManifest.failure_type?.replaceAll("_", " ")}
              </p>
              <span>Created: {formatDate(latestManifest.created_at)}</span>
            </div>

            <div className="manifest-status">
              <StatusBadge status={latestManifest.verification_status} />
              <strong>{formatSeconds(latestManifest.rto_seconds)}</strong>
              <span>Recorded RTO</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <EmptyState message="No recovery manifests have been recorded." />
        )}
      </section>

      {/* Recent activity */}
      <section className="dashboard-section">
        <div className="section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Recent disaster recovery activity</h2>
            <p>Latest recorded drill operations</p>
          </div>
          <Activity size={20} />
        </div>

        {drills.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="table-wrapper">
            <table className="dashboard-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Document</th>
                  <th>Failure type</th>
                  <th>Primary</th>
                  <th>Secondary</th>
                  <th>Status</th>
                  <th>Date</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>

              <tbody>
                {drills.slice(0, 8).map((drill, index) => (
                  <tr key={drill.id ?? index}>
                    <td>#{drill.document_id}</td>

                    <td>{drill.failure_type?.replaceAll("_", " ") || "N/A"}</td>

                    <td>
                      <StatusBadge
                        status={drill.primary_verified ? "VERIFIED" : "FAILED"}
                      />
                    </td>

                    <td>
                      <StatusBadge
                        status={
                          drill.secondary_verified ? "VERIFIED" : "FAILED"
                        }
                      />
                    </td>

                    <td>
                      <StatusBadge status={drill.drill_status} />
                    </td>

                    <td>{formatDate(drill.created_at)}</td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <EmptyState message="No disaster recovery activity is available." />
        )}

        {dashboard?.errors?.drills && (
          <p className="section-error">
            Drill history could not be retrieved. Your account may not have
            permission to view this information.
          </p>
        )}
      </section>

      {/* Quick actions */}
      <section className="dashboard-quick-actions">
        <div>
          <h2>Manage your documents</h2>
          <p>Continue with document upload and management.</p>
        </div>

        <Link to="/documents" className="primary-action">
          Open document management
          <ArrowUpRight size={17} />
        </Link>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

========================================
FILE: pages\Documents.jsx
========================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";

import {
  Search,
  Upload,
  Download,
  Eye,
  Trash2,
  RotateCcw,
  FileText,
  FileImage,
  File,
  RefreshCw,
  X,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  FolderOpen,
  AlertCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
} from "lucide-react";

import {
  getDocuments,
  uploadDocument,
  deleteDocument,
  restoreDocument,
  downloadDocument,
  previewDocument,
} from "../services/documentService";

import {
  formatFileSize,
  formatDocumentDate,
  getFileExtension,
  isPreviewable,
  isTextDocument,
  getDocumentStatus,
} from "../services/documentUtils";

import "../styles/documents.css";

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;
const SEARCH_DELAY = 400;

const EMPTY_FILTERS = {
  keyword: "",
  category: "",
  status: "",
};

const DOCUMENT_CATEGORIES = [
  { value: "GENERAL", label: "General" },
  { value: "CONTRACT", label: "Contract" },
  { value: "INVOICE", label: "Invoice" },
  { value: "LEGAL", label: "Legal" },
  { value: "FINANCIAL", label: "Financial" },
  { value: "OTHER", label: "Other" },
];

function areFiltersEqual(first, second) {
  return (
    first.keyword === second.keyword &&
    first.category === second.category &&
    first.status === second.status
  );
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// DOCUMENT ICON
// --------------------------------------------------

function DocumentIcon({ contentType }) {
  if (contentType?.toLowerCase().startsWith("image/")) {
    return <FileImage size={20} />;
  }

  if (contentType?.toLowerCase().includes("pdf")) {
    return <FileText size={20} />;
  }

  return <File size={20} />;
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// DOCUMENT STATUS
// --------------------------------------------------

function DocumentStatus({ status }) {
  const value = getDocumentStatus(status);

  const className =
    value === "ACTIVE" || value === "AVAILABLE"
      ? "document-status-active"
      : value === "DELETED"
        ? "document-status-deleted"
        : "document-status-neutral";

  return (
    <span className={`document-status ${className}`}>
      {value.replaceAll("_", " ")}
    </span>
  );
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// UPLOAD MODAL
// --------------------------------------------------

function UploadModal({ onClose, onUpload, uploading }) {
  const [file, setFile] = useState(null);
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState("");
  const [validationError, setValidationError] = useState("");

  function handleSubmit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();

    if (!file) {
      setValidationError("Please select a document.");
      return;
    }

    if (!category.trim()) {
      setValidationError("Please enter a document category.");
      return;
    }

    setValidationError("");

    onUpload(file, category.trim(), description.trim());
  }

  return (
    <div
      className="document-modal-overlay"
      onClick={uploading ? undefined : onClose}
    >
      <section
        className="document-modal"
        onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="upload-modal-title"
      >
        <div className="modal-heading">
          <div>
            <h2 id="upload-modal-title">Upload document</h2>
            <p>Add a new document to your evidence vault.</p>
          </div>

          <button
            className="icon-button"
            onClick={onClose}
            type="button"
            disabled={uploading}
            aria-label="Close upload modal"
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <label className="document-form-label">Select document</label>

          <label className="file-drop-area">
            <Upload size={28} />

            <strong>{file ? file.name : "Choose a file to upload"}</strong>

            <span>
              {file
                ? formatFileSize(file.size)
                : "Select a file from your computer"}
            </span>

            <input
              type="file"
              onChange={(event) => {
                setFile(event.target.files?.[0] || null);
                setValidationError("");
              }}
              disabled={uploading}
            />
          </label>

          <label className="document-form-label" htmlFor="document-category">
            Category <span>*</span>
          </label>

          <select
            id="document-category"
            className="document-input document-category-select"
            value={category}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setCategory(event.target.value);
              setValidationError("");
            }}
            required
            disabled={uploading}
          >
            <option value="">Select a category</option>

            {DOCUMENT_CATEGORIES.map((item) => (
              <option key={item.value} value={item.value}>
                {item.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          <label className="document-form-label">Description</label>

          <textarea
            className="document-input document-textarea"
            placeholder="Enter a short description..."
            value={description}
            onChange={(event) => setDescription(event.target.value)}
            rows={3}
            disabled={uploading}
          />

          {validationError && (
            <p className="document-validation-error">{validationError}</p>
          )}

          <div className="modal-actions">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="secondary-button"
              onClick={onClose}
              disabled={uploading}
            >
              Cancel
            </button>

            <button
              type="submit"
              className="document-primary-button"
              disabled={uploading}
            >
              <Upload size={16} />
              {uploading ? "Uploading..." : "Upload document"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// PREVIEW MODAL
// --------------------------------------------------

function PreviewModal({ document, previewUrl, previewText, onClose }) {
  const isImage = document.contentType?.toLowerCase().startsWith("image/");

  const isText = isTextDocument(
    document.contentType,
    document.originalFilename,
  );

  return (
    <div className="document-modal-overlay" onClick={onClose}>
      <section
        className="document-preview-modal"
        onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="preview-modal-title"
      >
        <div className="modal-heading">
          <div>
            <h2 id="preview-modal-title">{document.originalFilename}</h2>

            <p>Document preview</p>
          </div>

          <button
            className="icon-button"
            onClick={onClose}
            type="button"
            aria-label="Close preview"
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {isImage ? (
          <div className="image-preview-area">
            <img src={previewUrl} alt={document.originalFilename} />
          </div>
        ) : isText ? (
          <div className="text-preview-area">
            <pre>{previewText}</pre>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <iframe
            className="pdf-preview-frame"
            src={previewUrl}
            title={document.originalFilename}
          />
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// MAIN DOCUMENTS COMPONENT
// --------------------------------------------------

export default function Documents() {
  // Document data
  const [documents, setDocuments] = useState([]);
  const [page, setPage] = useState(0);
  const [totalPages, setTotalPages] = useState(0);
  const [totalElements, setTotalElements] = useState(0);

  // Search input values
  const [keyword, setKeyword] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");

  // Filters currently applied to the backend request
  const [appliedFilters, setAppliedFilters] = useState({
    ...EMPTY_FILTERS,
  });

  // Loading states
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [actionId, setActionId] = useState(null);

  // Modal states
  const [showUpload, setShowUpload] = useState(false);
  const [selectedPreviewDocument, setSelectedPreviewDocument] = useState(null);

  const [previewUrl, setPreviewUrl] = useState("");
  const [previewText, setPreviewText] = useState("");

  // Alerts
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  // Refresh counter forces a list reload after mutations.
  const [reloadKey, setReloadKey] = useState(0);

  // Prevents stale responses replacing newer results.
  const requestSequence = useRef(0);

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // REACTIVE SEARCH
  // --------------------------------------------------

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      const nextFilters = {
        keyword: keyword.trim(),
        category: category.trim(),
        status,
      };

      setAppliedFilters((previous) => {
        return areFiltersEqual(previous, nextFilters) ? previous : nextFilters;
      });

      setPage(0);
    }, SEARCH_DELAY);

    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [keyword, category, status]);

  // Search button applies the current values immediately.
  function applySearch(event) {
    event.preventDefault();

    const nextFilters = {
      keyword: keyword.trim(),
      category: category.trim(),
      status,
    };

    setAppliedFilters((previous) =>
      areFiltersEqual(previous, nextFilters) ? previous : nextFilters,
    );

    setPage(0);
  }

  function clearFilters() {
    setKeyword("");
    setCategory("");
    setStatus("");

    setAppliedFilters({
      ...EMPTY_FILTERS,
    });

    setPage(0);
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // LOAD DOCUMENTS
  // --------------------------------------------------

  useEffect(() => {
    const requestId = ++requestSequence.current;

    async function fetchDocuments() {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      try {
        const result = await getDocuments({
          ...appliedFilters,
          page,
          size: PAGE_SIZE,
        });

        if (requestId !== requestSequence.current) {
          return;
        }

        setDocuments(Array.isArray(result?.content) ? result.content : []);

        setTotalPages(result?.totalPages ?? 0);
        setTotalElements(result?.totalElements ?? 0);
      } catch (err) {
        if (requestId !== requestSequence.current) {
          return;
        }

        setDocuments([]);
        setTotalPages(0);
        setTotalElements(0);

        setError(err?.message || "Unable to load documents.");
      } finally {
        if (requestId === requestSequence.current) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    fetchDocuments();

    return () => {
      // Invalidates a response from an earlier request.
      requestSequence.current += 1;
    };
  }, [appliedFilters, page, reloadKey]);

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // PREVIEW URL CLEANUP
  // --------------------------------------------------

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (previewUrl) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(previewUrl);
      }
    };
  }, [previewUrl]);

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // SUMMARY
  // --------------------------------------------------

  const activeDocuments = useMemo(
    () =>
      documents.filter((item) => getDocumentStatus(item.status) !== "DELETED"),
    [documents],
  );

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // UPLOAD
  // --------------------------------------------------

  async function handleUpload(file, selectedCategory, description) {
    setUploading(true);
    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    try {
      await uploadDocument(file, selectedCategory, description);

      setShowUpload(false);

      // Reset search after a successful upload.
      setKeyword("");
      setCategory("");
      setStatus("");

      setAppliedFilters({
        ...EMPTY_FILTERS,
      });

      setPage(0);

      setMessage("Document uploaded successfully.");

      // Reload even when filters and page were already empty.
      setReloadKey((current) => current + 1);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || "Document upload failed.");
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // DOWNLOAD
  // --------------------------------------------------

  async function handleDownload(document) {
    setActionId(document.id);
    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    let objectUrl = "";

    try {
      const blob = await downloadDocument(document.id);

      objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

      const link = window.document.createElement("a");

      link.href = objectUrl;
      link.download = document.originalFilename || "document";

      window.document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      link.remove();

      setMessage("Document download started.");
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || "Download failed.");
    } finally {
      if (objectUrl) {
        window.setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl), 1000);
      }

      setActionId(null);
    }
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // PREVIEW
  // --------------------------------------------------

  async function handlePreview(document) {
    if (!isPreviewable(document.contentType, document.originalFilename)) {
      setError(
        "Preview is not supported for this file type. Please download the document instead.",
      );

      return;
    }

    setActionId(document.id);
    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    try {
      const blob = await previewDocument(document.id);

      if (isTextDocument(document.contentType, document.originalFilename)) {
        const text = await blob.text();

        setPreviewUrl("");
        setPreviewText(text);
      } else {
        const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

        setPreviewText("");
        setPreviewUrl(objectUrl);
      }

      setSelectedPreviewDocument(document);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || "Unable to preview document.");
    } finally {
      setActionId(null);
    }
  }

  function closePreview() {
    setSelectedPreviewDocument(null);
    setPreviewUrl("");
    setPreviewText("");
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // DELETE
  // --------------------------------------------------

  async function handleDelete(document) {
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      `Are you sure you want to delete "${document.originalFilename}"?`,
    );

    if (!confirmed) return;

    setActionId(document.id);
    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    try {
      await deleteDocument(document.id);

      setMessage("Document deleted successfully.");

      if (documents.length === 1 && page > 0) {
        setPage((current) => current - 1);
      } else {
        setReloadKey((current) => current + 1);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || "Unable to delete document.");
    } finally {
      setActionId(null);
    }
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // RESTORE
  // --------------------------------------------------

  async function handleRestore(document) {
    setActionId(document.id);
    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    try {
      await restoreDocument(document.id);

      setMessage("Document restored successfully.");

      setReloadKey((current) => current + 1);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || "Unable to restore document.");
    } finally {
      setActionId(null);
    }
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // RENDER
  // --------------------------------------------------

  return (
    <main className="documents-page">
      {/* Heading */}

      <section className="documents-heading">
        <div>
          <span className="documents-eyebrow">EVIDENCE VAULT</span>

          <h1>Document Management</h1>

          <p>Upload, organize, preview and manage your protected documents.</p>
        </div>

        <button
          className="document-primary-button"
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setShowUpload(true)}
        >
          <Upload size={17} />
          Upload document
        </button>
      </section>

      {/* Summary */}

      <section className="document-summary-grid">
        <div className="document-summary-card">
          <div className="document-summary-icon">
            <FolderOpen size={21} />
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Total records</span>
            <strong>{totalElements}</strong>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="document-summary-card">
          <div className="document-summary-icon summary-green">
            <CheckCircle2 size={21} />
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Active on this page</span>
            <strong>{activeDocuments.length}</strong>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="document-summary-card">
          <div className="document-summary-icon summary-purple">
            <FileText size={21} />
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Current page</span>

            <strong>
              {totalPages > 0 ? page + 1 : 0} / {totalPages}
            </strong>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Success alert */}

      {message && (
        <div className="document-alert document-alert-success">
          <CheckCircle2 size={17} />

          <span>{message}</span>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMessage("")}
            aria-label="Dismiss success message"
          >
            <X size={15} />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Error alert */}

      {error && (
        <div className="document-alert document-alert-error">
          <AlertCircle size={17} />

          <span>{error}</span>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setError("")}
            aria-label="Dismiss error message"
          >
            <X size={15} />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Search and filters */}

      <section className="document-toolbar">
        <form className="document-search-form" onSubmit={applySearch}>
          <div className="document-search-box">
            <Search size={18} />

            <input
              type="search"
              placeholder="Search document name..."
              value={keyword}
              onChange={(event) => setKeyword(event.target.value)}
            />
          </div>

          <select
            value={appliedFilters.category}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setAppliedFilters((previous) => ({
                ...previous,
                category: event.target.value,
              }));
              setPage(0);
            }}
          >
            <option value="">All Categories</option>

            {DOCUMENT_CATEGORIES.map((item) => (
              <option key={item.value} value={item.value}>
                {item.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          <select
            className="document-filter-input"
            value={status}
            onChange={(event) => setStatus(event.target.value)}
          >
            <option value="">All statuses</option>
            <option value="ACTIVE">Active</option>
            <option value="DELETED">Deleted</option>
          </select>

          <button
            type="submit"
            className="document-search-button"
            disabled={loading}
          >
            Search
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            className="document-clear-button"
            onClick={clearFilters}
          >
            Clear
          </button>
        </form>

        <button
          type="button"
          className="document-refresh-button"
          onClick={() => setReloadKey((current) => current + 1)}
          disabled={loading}
          title="Refresh documents"
          aria-label="Refresh documents"
        >
          <RefreshCw size={17} className={loading ? "documents-spin" : ""} />
        </button>
      </section>

      {/* Documents table */}

      <section className="documents-table-card">
        <div className="documents-table-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Your documents</h2>

            <p>
              Showing {documents.length} of {totalElements} records
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="documents-empty-state">
            <RefreshCw className="documents-spin" size={25} />

            <p>Loading documents...</p>
          </div>
        ) : documents.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="documents-empty-state">
            <FolderOpen size={35} />

            <h3>No documents found</h3>

            <p>Try changing your search filters or upload a new document.</p>

            <button
              type="button"
              className="document-primary-button"
              onClick={() => setShowUpload(true)}
            >
              <Upload size={16} />
              Upload document
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="documents-table-scroll">
            <table className="documents-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Document</th>
                  <th>Category</th>
                  <th>Size</th>
                  <th>Checksum</th>
                  <th>Status</th>
                  <th>Uploaded by</th>
                  <th>Upload date</th>
                  <th>Actions</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>

              <tbody>
                {documents.map((document) => {
                  const deleted =
                    getDocumentStatus(document.status) === "DELETED";

                  const rowLoading = actionId === document.id;

                  return (
                    <tr key={document.id}>
                      <td>
                        <div className="document-name-cell">
                          <div className="document-file-icon">
                            <DocumentIcon contentType={document.contentType} />
                          </div>

                          <div>
                            <strong>{document.originalFilename}</strong>

                            <span>
                              {getFileExtension(document.originalFilename)}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      <td>{document.category || "N/A"}</td>

                      <td>{formatFileSize(document.fileSizeBytes)}</td>

                      <td>
                        <span
                          className="checksum-value"
                          title={document.sha256Checksum || ""}
                        >
                          {document.sha256Checksum
                            ? `${document.sha256Checksum.slice(0, 12)}...`
                            : "N/A"}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      <td>
                        <DocumentStatus status={document.status} />
                      </td>

                      <td>{document.uploadedBy || "N/A"}</td>

                      <td>{formatDocumentDate(document.createdAt)}</td>

                      <td>
                        <div className="document-row-actions">
                          {!deleted && (
                            <>
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                title="Preview"
                                aria-label={`Preview ${document.originalFilename}`}
                                disabled={
                                  rowLoading ||
                                  !isPreviewable(
                                    document.contentType,
                                    document.originalFilename,
                                  )
                                }
                                onClick={() => handlePreview(document)}
                              >
                                <Eye size={16} />
                              </button>

                              <button
                                type="button"
                                title="Download"
                                aria-label={`Download ${document.originalFilename}`}
                                disabled={rowLoading}
                                onClick={() => handleDownload(document)}
                              >
                                <Download size={16} />
                              </button>

                              <button
                                type="button"
                                title="Delete"
                                aria-label={`Delete ${document.originalFilename}`}
                                className="delete-action"
                                disabled={rowLoading}
                                onClick={() => handleDelete(document)}
                              >
                                <Trash2 size={16} />
                              </button>
                            </>
                          )}

                          {deleted && (
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              title="Restore"
                              aria-label={`Restore ${document.originalFilename}`}
                              className="restore-action"
                              disabled={rowLoading}
                              onClick={() => handleRestore(document)}
                            >
                              <RotateCcw size={16} />
                            </button>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Pagination */}

        <div className="documents-pagination">
          <span>
            Page {totalPages > 0 ? page + 1 : 0} of {totalPages}
          </span>

          <div>
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={page <= 0 || loading}
              onClick={() => setPage((current) => current - 1)}
            >
              <ChevronLeft size={17} />
              Previous
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={loading || totalPages === 0 || page >= totalPages - 1}
              onClick={() => setPage((current) => current + 1)}
            >
              Next
              <ChevronRight size={17} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Upload modal */}

      {showUpload && (
        <UploadModal
          onClose={() => setShowUpload(false)}
          onUpload={handleUpload}
          uploading={uploading}
        />
      )}

      {/* Preview modal */}

      {selectedPreviewDocument && (
        <PreviewModal
          document={selectedPreviewDocument}
          previewUrl={previewUrl}
          previewText={previewText}
          onClose={closePreview}
        />
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

========================================
FILE: pages\Home.jsx
========================================
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { ShieldCheck, ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import { ROUTES } from "../config/constants";

import { useState } from "react";

import {
  ShieldCheck,
  User,
  Lock,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  ArrowRight,
  AlertCircle,
} from "lucide-react";

import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";

import "../styles/login.css";

function Login() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();

  const { login, loading } = useAuth();

  const [credentials, setCredentials] = useState({
    username: "",
    password: "",
  });

  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const handleChange = (event) => {
    const { name, value } = event.target;

    setCredentials((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      [name]: value,
    }));
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");

    if (!credentials.username.trim() || !credentials.password) {
      setError("Please enter both username and password.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      await login(credentials);

      const destination = location.state?.from?.pathname || "/dashboard";

      navigate(destination, { replace: true });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Login failed. Please try again.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="login-page">
      <div className="login-decoration"></div>

      <div className="login-container">
        <div className="login-brand-icon">
          <ShieldCheck size={38} />
        </div>

        <div className="login-heading">
          <h1>Welcome Back</h1>

          <p>Sign in to access your secure evidence vault.</p>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="login-error">
            <AlertCircle size={17} />
            <span>{error}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <div className="form-group">
            <label htmlFor="username">Username</label>

            <div className="input-wrapper">
              <User size={18} />

              <input
                id="username"
                name="username"
                type="text"
                placeholder="Enter your username"
                value={credentials.username}
                onChange={handleChange}
                autoComplete="username"
                required
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="form-group">
            <label htmlFor="password">Password</label>

            <div className="input-wrapper">
              <Lock size={18} />

              <input
                id="password"
                name="password"
                type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                placeholder="Enter your password"
                value={credentials.password}
                onChange={handleChange}
                autoComplete="current-password"
                required
              />

              <button
                type="button"
                className="password-toggle"
                onClick={() => setShowPassword((previous) => !previous)}
                aria-label={showPassword ? "Hide password" : "Show password"}
              >
                {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={18} /> : <Eye size={18} />}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button type="submit" className="login-submit" disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? "Signing In..." : "Sign In"}

            {!loading && <ArrowRight size={18} />}
          </button>
        </form>

        <div className="login-security">
          <ShieldCheck size={15} />

          <span>Secure authentication powered by JWT</span>
        </div>

        <Link to="/" className="back-home">
          Back to Home
        </Link>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default Login;
