import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  Activity,
  AlertCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
  Cloud,
  Database,
  FileText,
  RefreshCw,
  ShieldCheck,
  XCircle,
  Zap,
} from "lucide-react";

import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { USER_ROLES } from "../config/constants";

import { getDocuments } from "../services/documentService";
import {
  getDrillHistory,
  getRecoveryManifests,
} from "../services/dashboardService";

import {
  verifyDocument,
  runRecoveryDrill,
  simulatePrimaryFailure,
  simulateCorruption,
} from "../services/recoveryService";

import "../styles/recovery.css";

function Recovery() {
  const { hasRole } = useAuth();
  const isAdmin = hasRole([USER_ROLES.ADMIN]);

  const [documents, setDocuments] = useState([]);
  const [selectedDocumentId, setSelectedDocumentId] = useState("");

  const [history, setHistory] = useState([]);
  const [manifests, setManifests] = useState([]);

  const [verification, setVerification] = useState(null);
  const [operationResult, setOperationResult] = useState(null);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [working, setWorking] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const loadInitialData = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const documentResponse = await getDocuments({
        page: 0,
        size: 100,
      });

      const records = documentResponse?.content || [];
      setDocuments(records);

      if (records.length > 0) {
        setSelectedDocumentId((current) => current || String(records[0].id));
      }

      // Only Admin can access these read-only endpoints.
      if (isAdmin) {
        const results = await Promise.allSettled([
          getDrillHistory(),
          getRecoveryManifests(),
        ]);

        setHistory(
          results[0].status === "fulfilled" && Array.isArray(results[0].value)
            ? results[0].value
            : [],
        );

        setManifests(
          results[1].status === "fulfilled" && Array.isArray(results[1].value)
            ? results[1].value
            : [],
        );
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Unable to load recovery data.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [isAdmin]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadInitialData();
  }, [loadInitialData]);

  const refreshHistory = async () => {
    if (!isAdmin) return;

    const results = await Promise.allSettled([
      getDrillHistory(),
      getRecoveryManifests(),
    ]);

    if (results[0].status === "fulfilled") {
      setHistory(Array.isArray(results[0].value) ? results[0].value : []);
    }

    if (results[1].status === "fulfilled") {
      setManifests(Array.isArray(results[1].value) ? results[1].value : []);
    }
  };

  const executeOperation = async (operation, confirmation) => {
    if (!selectedDocumentId || working) return;

    const confirmed = window.confirm(confirmation);

    if (!confirmed) return;

    setWorking(true);
    setError("");
    setOperationResult(null);

    try {
      const result = await operation(Number(selectedDocumentId));

      setOperationResult(result);

      // Refresh the read-only verification and history after the action.
      const verified = await verifyDocument(Number(selectedDocumentId));

      setVerification(verified);

      await refreshHistory();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Recovery operation failed.");
    } finally {
      setWorking(false);
    }
  };

  const handleVerify = async () => {
    if (!selectedDocumentId || working) return;

    setWorking(true);
    setError("");
    setOperationResult(null);

    try {
      const result = await verifyDocument(Number(selectedDocumentId));

      setVerification(result);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Document verification failed.");
      setVerification(null);
    } finally {
      setWorking(false);
    }
  };

  const formatDate = (value) => {
    if (!value) return "â€”";

    const date = new Date(value);

    return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? value : date.toLocaleString();
  };

  const formatSeconds = (value) => {
    if (value === null || value === undefined) return "â€”";
    return `${Number(value).toFixed(3)} sec`;
  };

  const selectedDocument = documents.find(
    (document) => String(document.id) === selectedDocumentId,
  );

  const primary = verification?.primary;
  const secondary = verification?.secondary;

  const Status = ({ value }) => {
    const healthy = value === true;

    return (
      <span className={`recovery-status ${healthy ? "healthy" : "unhealthy"}`}>
        {healthy ? <CheckCircle2 size={15} /> : <XCircle size={15} />}
        {healthy ? "Verified" : "Not verified"}
      </span>
    );
  };

  const drill = operationResult?.drill || operationResult;

  return (
    <main className="recovery-page">
      <header className="recovery-header">
        <div>
          <div className="recovery-title">
            <ShieldCheck size={29} />
            <h1>Disaster Recovery</h1>
          </div>

          <p>
            Verify regional replicas and execute controlled recovery drills.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          className="recovery-refresh"
          onClick={loadInitialData}
          disabled={loading || working}
        >
          <RefreshCw size={16} />
          Refresh
        </button>
      </header>

      <section className="recovery-region-grid">
        <article className="recovery-region-card">
          <div className="recovery-region-icon">
            <Cloud size={23} />
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Primary Region</span>
            <h3>Mumbai</h3>
            <p>ap-south-1</p>
          </div>
        </article>

        <article className="recovery-region-card">
          <div className="recovery-region-icon secondary-region-icon">
            <Database size={23} />
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Secondary Region</span>
            <h3>Singapore</h3>
            <p>ap-southeast-1</p>
          </div>
        </article>
      </section>

      <section className="recovery-selector-card">
        <label htmlFor="recovery-document">Select Document</label>

        <div className="recovery-selector-row">
          <select
            id="recovery-document"
            value={selectedDocumentId}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setSelectedDocumentId(event.target.value);
              setVerification(null);
              setOperationResult(null);
              setError("");
            }}
            disabled={loading || documents.length === 0 || working}
          >
            {documents.length === 0 && (
              <option value="">No documents available</option>
            )}

            {documents.map((document) => (
              <option key={document.id} value={String(document.id)}>
                #{document.id} â€” {document.originalFilename}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          <button
            className="recovery-verify-button"
            onClick={handleVerify}
            disabled={!selectedDocumentId || working || loading}
          >
            <ShieldCheck size={16} />
            Verify
          </button>
        </div>

        {selectedDocument && (
          <p className="recovery-selected-info">
            <FileText size={15} />
            {selectedDocument.originalFilename}
            {" Â· "}
            {selectedDocument.category}
            {" Â· "}
            {selectedDocument.status}
          </p>
        )}
      </section>

      {error && (
        <div className="recovery-error">
          <AlertCircle size={18} />
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      {working && (
        <div className="recovery-working">
          <RefreshCw className="recovery-spin" size={19} />
          Recovery operation is running. Please wait...
        </div>
      )}

      <section className="recovery-verification">
        <div className="recovery-section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Regional Verification</h2>
            <p>Integrity status of the selected document.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="recovery-verification-grid">
          <article className="recovery-verification-card">
            <Cloud size={23} />
            <h3>Mumbai â€” Primary</h3>
            <Status value={primary?.verified === true} />

            {primary && (
              <>
                <p>Object exists: {String(primary.exists)}</p>
                <p>Checksum verified: {String(primary.checksum_verified)}</p>
                <p>Size verified: {String(primary.size_verified)}</p>
              </>
            )}
          </article>

          <article className="recovery-verification-card">
            <Database size={23} />
            <h3>Singapore â€” Secondary</h3>
            <Status value={secondary?.verified === true} />

            {secondary && (
              <>
                <p>Object exists: {String(secondary.exists)}</p>
                <p>Checksum verified: {String(secondary.checksum_verified)}</p>
                <p>Size verified: {String(secondary.size_verified)}</p>
              </>
            )}
          </article>
        </div>

        {verification && (
          <div className="recovery-overall">
            Overall verification:
            <Status value={verification.overall_verified === true} />
          </div>
        )}

        {!verification && !loading && (
          <p className="recovery-hint">
            Select a document and click Verify to check both regions.
          </p>
        )}
      </section>

      <section className="recovery-actions-section">
        <div className="recovery-section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Recovery Operations</h2>
            <p>Run controlled recovery tests for the selected document.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="recovery-action-grid">
          <article className="recovery-action-card">
            <div className="recovery-action-icon">
              <Activity size={22} />
            </div>

            <h3>Run Recovery Drill</h3>
            <p>
              Verify both replicas and recover automatically if the primary copy
              is not verified and the secondary is healthy.
            </p>

            <button
              onClick={() =>
                executeOperation(
                  runRecoveryDrill,
                  "Run the recovery drill for this document?",
                )
              }
              disabled={!selectedDocumentId || working}
            >
              Run Drill
            </button>
          </article>

          <article className="recovery-action-card">
            <div className="recovery-action-icon">
              <Cloud size={22} />
            </div>

            <h3>Simulate Primary Failure</h3>
            <p>
              Assume Mumbai is unavailable and recover from the verified
              Singapore replica.
            </p>

            <button
              onClick={() =>
                executeOperation(
                  simulatePrimaryFailure,
                  "Run a primary-region failure simulation? This operation can create a new primary S3 version.",
                )
              }
              disabled={!selectedDocumentId || working}
            >
              Simulate Failure
            </button>
          </article>

          <article className="recovery-action-card">
            <div className="recovery-action-icon">
              <Zap size={22} />
            </div>

            <h3>Simulate Corruption</h3>
            <p>
              Simulate a checksum mismatch in memory and attempt recovery from
              the verified secondary replica.
            </p>

            <button
              onClick={() =>
                executeOperation(
                  simulateCorruption,
                  "Run the corruption simulation? Recovery can write a new primary S3 version.",
                )
              }
              disabled={!selectedDocumentId || working}
            >
              Simulate Corruption
            </button>
          </article>
        </div>
      </section>

      {operationResult && (
        <section className="recovery-result-section">
          <div className="recovery-section-heading">
            <div>
              <h2>Latest Operation Result</h2>
              <p>Response returned by the recovery service.</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="recovery-result-grid">
            <div>
              <span>Drill Status</span>
              <strong>{drill?.drill_status || "Completed"}</strong>
            </div>

            <div>
              <span>Recovery Performed</span>
              <strong>{String(drill?.recovery_performed ?? "â€”")}</strong>
            </div>

            <div>
              <span>Recovery Time (RTO)</span>
              <strong>{formatSeconds(drill?.rto_seconds)}</strong>
            </div>

            <div>
              <span>Replication Lag</span>
              <strong>{formatSeconds(drill?.replication_lag_seconds)}</strong>
            </div>
          </div>

          <details className="recovery-raw-result">
            <summary>View complete operation response</summary>
            <pre>{JSON.stringify(operationResult, null, 2)}</pre>
          </details>
        </section>
      )}

      {isAdmin && (
        <>
          <section className="recovery-history-section">
            <div className="recovery-section-heading">
              <div>
                <h2>Drill History</h2>
                <p>Previously recorded recovery operations.</p>
              </div>

              <span>{history.length} records</span>
            </div>

            {history.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="recovery-empty">
                No recovery drills have been recorded yet.
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="recovery-table-wrapper">
                <table className="recovery-table">
                  <thead>
                    <tr>
                      <th>Document</th>
                      <th>Failure Type</th>
                      <th>Status</th>
                      <th>Primary</th>
                      <th>Secondary</th>
                      <th>RTO</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>

                  <tbody>
                    {history.map((item, index) => (
                      <tr key={item.id ?? index}>
                        <td>#{item.document_id}</td>
                        <td>{item.failure_type || "â€”"}</td>
                        <td>{item.drill_status || "â€”"}</td>
                        <td>{String(item.primary_verified ?? "â€”")}</td>
                        <td>{String(item.secondary_verified ?? "â€”")}</td>
                        <td>{formatSeconds(item.rto_seconds)}</td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            )}
          </section>

          <section className="recovery-history-section">
            <div className="recovery-section-heading">
              <div>
                <h2>Recovery Manifests</h2>
                <p>Recorded evidence of recovery operations.</p>
              </div>

              <span>{manifests.length} records</span>
            </div>

            {manifests.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="recovery-empty">
                No recovery manifests are available.
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="recovery-table-wrapper">
                <table className="recovery-table">
                  <thead>
                    <tr>
                      <th>Document</th>
                      <th>Failure Type</th>
                      <th>Source Region</th>
                      <th>Destination Region</th>
                      <th>Verification</th>
                      <th>RTO</th>
                      <th>Created At</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>

                  <tbody>
                    {manifests.map((item, index) => (
                      <tr key={item.id ?? index}>
                        <td>#{item.document_id}</td>
                        <td>{item.failure_type || "â€”"}</td>
                        <td>{item.source_region || "â€”"}</td>
                        <td>{item.destination_region || "â€”"}</td>
                        <td>{item.verification_status || "â€”"}</td>

                      </tr>

        </>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

import { ShieldAlert } from "lucide-react";

import { APP_CONFIG } from "../config/constants";

const API_BASE_URL = APP_CONFIG.API_BASE_URL;

async function readResponseBody(response) {
  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") || "";

  try {
    // Handles application/json and application/problem+json.
    if (contentType.toLowerCase().includes("json")) {
      return await response.json();
    }

    return await response.text();
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function extractMessage(data) {
  if (!data) return "";

  if (typeof data === "string") {
    const trimmed = data.trim();

    // Avoid showing a JSON response as raw text.
    if (trimmed.startsWith("{") || trimmed.startsWith("[")) {
      try {
        return extractMessage(JSON.parse(trimmed));
      } catch {
        return trimmed;
      }
    }

    return trimmed;
  }

  if (Array.isArray(data)) {
    return data
      .map((item) => {
        if (typeof item === "string") return item;
        return item?.defaultMessage || item?.message || "";
      })
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(", ");
  }

  if (typeof data === "object") {
    const detail = data.detail;

    if (typeof detail === "string") return detail;

    if (Array.isArray(detail)) {
      return extractMessage(detail);
    }

    return (
      data.message ||
      data.error ||
      data.title ||
      ""
    );
  }

  return "";
}

export function createApiErrorMessage(
  response,
  responseData,
  endpoint = "",
  customFallback = ""
) {
  const status = response.status;

  if (status === 401) {
    if (endpoint.includes("/auth/login")) {
      return "Invalid username or password. Please check your credentials and try again.";
    }

    return "Your session has expired. Please sign in again.";
  }

  if (status === 403) {
    return "You do not have permission to perform this action.";
  }

  if (status === 404) {
    return "The requested resource could not be found.";
  }

  if (status === 409) {
    return "This operation cannot be completed because the resource is in its current state.";
  }

  if (status === 413) {
    return "The selected file is too large to upload.";
  }

  if (status === 415) {
    return "This file format is not supported by the server.";
  }

  if (status === 422) {
    return (
      extractMessage(responseData) ||
      "Some of the submitted information is invalid. Please check your inputs."
    );
  }

  if (status >= 500) {
    return (
      customFallback ||
      "The server encountered an error. Please try again later."
    );
  }

  return (
    extractMessage(responseData) ||
    customFallback ||
    "The request could not be completed. Please try again."
  );
}

export async function getApiErrorMessage(
  response,
  fallback = "",
  endpoint = ""
) {
  const responseData = await readResponseBody(response);

  return createApiErrorMessage(
    response,
    responseData,
    endpoint,
    fallback
  );
}

export async function apiRequest(endpoint, options = {}) {
  const token = localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-token");

  const headers = {
    ...options.headers,
  };

  const isFormData = options.body instanceof FormData;

  // JSON requests need Content-Type.
  // FormData must NOT have a manually assigned Content-Type:
  // the browser adds the multipart boundary automatically.
  if (
    options.body !== undefined &&
    !isFormData &&
    !headers["Content-Type"]
  ) {
    headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json";
  }

  // Do not attach an old JWT to the login request.
  if (token && !endpoint.includes("/auth/login")) {
    headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
  }

  let response;

  try {
    response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${endpoint}`, {
      ...options,
      headers,
    });
  } catch {
    throw new Error(
      "Unable to connect to the server. Check that the backend is running and try again."
    );
  }

  if (response.status === 204) {
    return null;
  }

  const responseData = await readResponseBody(response);

  if (!response.ok) {
    const message = createApiErrorMessage(
      response,
      responseData,
      endpoint
    );

    throw new Error(message);
  }

  return responseData;
}

import { apiRequest } from "./api";

export async function loginUser(credentials) {
  const response = await apiRequest("/v1/auth/login", {
    method: "POST",
    body: JSON.stringify({
      username: credentials.username,
      password: credentials.password,
    }),
  });

  return response;
}

export function saveAuthSession(authData) {
  localStorage.setItem("evidence-vault-token", authData.accessToken);

  localStorage.setItem(
    "evidence-vault-user",
    JSON.stringify({
      username: authData.username,
      roles: authData.roles,
      tokenType: authData.tokenType,
      expiresIn: authData.expiresIn,
    }),
  );
}

export function getStoredUser() {
  const storedUser = localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-user");

  if (!storedUser) {
    return null;
  }

  try {
    return JSON.parse(storedUser);
  } catch {
    clearAuthSession();
    return null;
  }
}

export function clearAuthSession() {
  localStorage.removeItem("evidence-vault-token");
  localStorage.removeItem("evidence-vault-user");
}

import { apiRequest } from "./api";

export async function getSystemStatus() {
  return apiRequest("/v1/system/status");
}

export async function getRecoveryMetrics() {
  return apiRequest("/v1/dr/metrics");
}

export async function getDrillHistory() {
  return apiRequest("/v1/dr/drills");
}

export async function getRecoveryManifests() {
  return apiRequest("/v1/dr/manifests");
}

export async function getDocumentSummary() {
  return apiRequest("/v1/documents?page=0&size=1");
}

export async function getDashboardData() {
  const requests = await Promise.allSettled([
    getSystemStatus(),
    getRecoveryMetrics(),
    getDrillHistory(),
    getRecoveryManifests(),
    getDocumentSummary(),
  ]);

  const getValue = (index, fallback) =>
    requests[index].status === "fulfilled" ? requests[index].value : fallback;

  const getError = (index) =>
    requests[index].status === "rejected"
      ? requests[index].reason?.message || "Request failed"
      : null;

  return {
    system: getValue(0, null),
    metrics: getValue(1, []),
    drills: getValue(2, []),
    manifests: getValue(3, []),
    documents: getValue(4, null),

    errors: {
      system: getError(0),
      metrics: getError(1),
      drills: getError(2),
      manifests: getError(3),
      documents: getError(4),
    },
  };
}

FILE: services\dashboardUtils.js

export function normalizeArray(data) {
  return Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
}
export function average(values) {
  const valid = values
    .filter((value) => value !== null && value !== undefined && value !== "")
    .map(Number)
    .filter(Number.isFinite);

  if (!valid.length) return null;

  return Number(
    (valid.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / valid.length).toFixed(3),
  );
}

export function calculateDrillSummary(drills) {
  const total = drills.length;

  const successful = drills.filter(
    (item) => item.drill_status === "SUCCESS",
  ).length;

  const failed = drills.filter((item) => item.drill_status === "FAILED").length;

  const attentionRequired = drills.filter(
    (item) => item.drill_status === "RECOVERY_REQUIRED",
  ).length;

  const recoveryOperations = drills.filter(
    (item) => item.recovery_performed === true,
  ).length;

  const corruptionDrills = drills.filter(
    (item) =>
      item.corruption_detected === true ||
      item.failure_type === "SIMULATED_CORRUPTION",
  ).length;

  const primaryFailures = drills.filter(
    (item) => item.failure_type === "SIMULATED_PRIMARY_FAILURE",
  ).length;

  const checksumMismatches = drills.filter((item) => {
    const primaryMismatch =
      item.primary_checksum &&
      item.expected_checksum &&
      item.primary_checksum !== item.expected_checksum;

    const secondaryMismatch =
      item.secondary_checksum &&
      item.expected_checksum &&
      item.secondary_checksum !== item.expected_checksum;

    return primaryMismatch || secondaryMismatch;
  }).length;

  return {
    total,
    successful,
    failed,
    attentionRequired,
    recoveryOperations,
    corruptionDrills,
    primaryFailures,
    checksumMismatches,

    successRate: total ? Number(((successful / total) * 100).toFixed(1)) : null,
  };
}

export function calculateMetricSummary(metrics) {
  const rtoValues = metrics.map(
    (item) => item.recovery_rto_seconds ?? item.recovery_workflow_seconds,
  );

  const rpoValues = metrics
    .map((item) => item.estimated_rpo_seconds)
    .filter((value) => value !== null && value !== undefined);

  const replicationCompleted = metrics.filter(
    (item) => item.replication_status === "COMPLETED",
  ).length;

  const replicationFailed = metrics.filter(
    (item) => item.replication_status === "FAILED",
  ).length;

  const replicationTimedOut = metrics.filter(
    (item) => item.replication_timed_out === true,
  ).length;

  return {
    averageRto: average(rtoValues),
    averageRpo: average(rpoValues),
    replicationCompleted,
    replicationFailed,
    replicationTimedOut,
  };
}

export function getLatestRecord(records) {
  if (!records.length) return null;

  return [...records].sort(
    (a, b) => new Date(b.created_at) - new Date(a.created_at),
  )[0];
}

export function formatSeconds(value) {
  if (
    value === null ||
    value === undefined ||
    !Number.isFinite(Number(value))
  ) {
    return "N/A";
  }

  return `${Number(value).toFixed(3)} sec`;
}

export function formatPercentage(value) {
  return value === null || value === undefined ? "N/A" : `${value}%`;
}

export function formatDate(value) {
  if (!value) return "N/A";

  const date = new Date(value);

  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? "N/A" : date.toLocaleString();
}

FILE: services\documentFileService.js

import { APP_CONFIG } from "../config/constants";

const API_BASE_URL = APP_CONFIG.API_BASE_URL;

function getFriendlyError(status) {
  switch (status) {
    case 401:
      return "Your session has expired. Please sign in again.";
    case 403:
      return "You don't have permission to access this document.";
    case 404:
      return "The document could not be found.";
    case 500:
      return "The server couldn't retrieve this document. Please try again.";
    default:
      return "Unable to retrieve the document. Please try again.";
  }
}

export async function fetchDocumentBlob(documentId) {
  const token = localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-token");

  if (!token) {
    throw new Error("Your session has expired. Please sign in again.");
  }

  const response = await fetch(
    `${API_BASE_URL}/v1/documents/${documentId}/download`,
    {
      method: "GET",
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      },
    },
  );

  if (!response.ok) {
    let message = getFriendlyError(response.status);

    try {
      const errorData = await response.json();

      // Don't expose raw server exceptions to the UI.
      if (response.status >= 500) {
        message =
          "The server couldn't retrieve this document. Please try again.";
      } else if (response.status === 401 || response.status === 403) {
        message = getFriendlyError(response.status);
      } else if (errorData.message) {
        message = errorData.message;
      }
    } catch {
      // Keep the friendly fallback message.
    }

    throw new Error(message);
  }

  return await response.blob();
}

export function downloadBlob(blob, filename) {
  const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

  const link = document.createElement("a");
  link.href = objectUrl;
  link.download = filename || "document";
  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  link.remove();

  URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl);
}

import { apiRequest, getApiErrorMessage } from "./api";

import { APP_CONFIG } from "../config/constants";

const DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT = "/v1/documents";
const API_BASE_URL = APP_CONFIG.API_BASE_URL;

function getAuthToken() {
  const token = localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-token");

  if (!token) {
    throw new Error("Your session has expired. Please sign in again.");
  }

  return token;
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// GET DOCUMENTS
// --------------------------------------------------

export async function getDocuments({
  keyword = "",
  category = "",
  status = "",
  page = 0,
  size = APP_CONFIG.DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE,
} = {}) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();

  params.set("page", String(page));
  params.set("size", String(size));

  if (keyword.trim()) {
    params.set("keyword", keyword.trim());
  }

  if (category.trim()) {
    params.set("category", category.trim());
  }

  if (status.trim()) {
    params.set("status", status.trim());
  }

  return apiRequest(`${DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT}?${params.toString()}`);
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// UPLOAD DOCUMENT
// --------------------------------------------------

export async function uploadDocument(file, category, description = "") {
  if (!(file instanceof File)) {
    throw new Error("Please select a valid document.");
  }

  if (!category?.trim()) {
    throw new Error("Please provide a document category.");
  }

  const formData = new FormData();

  // These names must match the Spring Boot upload endpoint.
  formData.append("file", file);
  formData.append("category", category.trim());

  if (description?.trim()) {
    formData.append("description", description.trim());
  }

  return apiRequest(`${DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT}/upload`, {
    method: "POST",
    body: formData,
  });
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// DELETE DOCUMENT
// --------------------------------------------------

export async function deleteDocument(documentId) {
  if (!documentId) {
    throw new Error("A valid document ID is required.");
  }

  return apiRequest(`${DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT}/${encodeURIComponent(documentId)}`, {
    method: "DELETE",
  });
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// RESTORE DOCUMENT
// --------------------------------------------------

export async function restoreDocument(documentId) {
  if (!documentId) {
    throw new Error("A valid document ID is required.");
  }

  return apiRequest(
    `${DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT}/${encodeURIComponent(documentId)}/restore`,
    {
      method: "POST",
    },
  );
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// COMMON PREVIEW / DOWNLOAD REQUEST
// --------------------------------------------------

async function fetchDocumentFile(documentId, action) {
  if (!documentId) {
    throw new Error("A valid document ID is required.");
  }

  if (!["preview", "download"].includes(action)) {
    throw new Error("Unsupported document operation.");
  }

  const token = getAuthToken();

  const endpoint =
    `${DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT}/` + `${encodeURIComponent(documentId)}/${action}`;

  let response;

  try {
    response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${endpoint}`, {
      method: "GET",
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        Accept: "*/*",
      },
      cache: "no-store",
    });
  } catch {
    throw new Error(
      `Unable to connect to the server to ${action} this document.`,
    );
  }

  if (!response.ok) {
    const fallback =
      action === "preview"
        ? "The server couldn't preview this document. Please try again later."
        : "The server couldn't download this document. Please try again later.";

    const message = await getApiErrorMessage(response, fallback, endpoint);

    throw new Error(message);
  }

  return response.blob();
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// PREVIEW DOCUMENT
// --------------------------------------------------

export function previewDocument(documentId) {
  return fetchDocumentFile(documentId, "preview");
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// DOWNLOAD DOCUMENT
// --------------------------------------------------

export function downloadDocument(documentId) {
  return fetchDocumentFile(documentId, "download");
}

FILE: services\documentUtils.js

export function formatFileSize(bytes) {
  if (bytes === null || bytes === undefined) {
    return "N/A";
  }

  const size = Number(bytes);

  if (!Number.isFinite(size) || size < 0) {
    return "N/A";
  }

  if (size === 0) {
    return "0 Bytes";
  }

  const units = ["Bytes", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"];

  const index = Math.min(
    Math.floor(Math.log(size) / Math.log(1024)),
    units.length - 1,
  );

  return `${(size / Math.pow(1024, index)).toFixed(2)} ${units[index]}`;
}

export function formatDocumentDate(value) {
  if (!value) {
    return "N/A";
  }

  const date = new Date(value);

  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? "N/A" : date.toLocaleString();
}

export function getFileExtension(filename = "") {
  if (!filename || !filename.includes(".")) {
    return "FILE";
  }

  return filename.split(".").pop().toUpperCase();
}

export function getNormalizedContentType(contentType = "") {
  return String(contentType).toLowerCase().split(";")[0].trim();
}

export function isTextDocument(contentType = "", filename = "") {
  const type = getNormalizedContentType(contentType);

  const extension = String(filename).toLowerCase().split(".").pop();

  const textExtensions = ["txt", "csv", "json", "xml", "log", "md"];

  return (
    type.startsWith("text/") ||
    ["application/json", "application/xml"].includes(type) ||
    textExtensions.includes(extension)
  );
}

export function isPreviewable(contentType = "", filename = "") {
  const type = getNormalizedContentType(contentType);

  const extension = String(filename).toLowerCase().split(".").pop();

  const supportedTypes = [
    "application/pdf",
    "image/png",
    "image/jpeg",
    "image/webp",
    "image/gif",
  ];

  const supportedExtensions = [
    "pdf",
    "png",
    "jpg",
    "jpeg",
    "webp",
    "gif",
    "txt",
    "csv",
    "json",
    "xml",
    "log",
    "md",
  ];

  return (
    supportedTypes.includes(type) ||
    type.startsWith("image/") ||
    isTextDocument(type, filename) ||
    supportedExtensions.includes(extension)
  );
}

export function getDocumentStatus(status) {
  return String(status || "UNKNOWN").toUpperCase();
}

========================================
FILE: App.jsx
========================================
import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route } from "react-router-dom";

import { ThemeProvider } from "./context/ThemeContext";
import { AuthProvider } from "./context/AuthContext";

import { USER_ROLES } from "./config/constants";

import Layout from "./components/Layout";
import ProtectedRoute from "./components/ProtectedRoute";

import Home from "./pages/Home";
import Login from "./pages/Login";
import Dashboard from "./pages/Dashboard";
import Documents from "./pages/Documents";
import Recovery from "./pages/Recovery";
import Audit from "./pages/Audit";
import Unauthorized from "./pages/Unauthorized";

function App() {
  return (
    <ThemeProvider>
      <AuthProvider>
        <BrowserRouter>
          <Routes>
            <Route element={<Layout />}>
              {/* Public pages */}
              <Route path="/" element={<Home />} />
              <Route path="/login" element={<Login />} />
              <Route path="/unauthorized" element={<Unauthorized />} />

              {/* All authenticated users */}
              <Route element={<ProtectedRoute />}>
                <Route path="/dashboard" element={<Dashboard />} />

                <Route path="/documents" element={<Documents />} />
              </Route>

              {/* Admin and Operator */}
              <Route
                element={
                  <ProtectedRoute
                    allowedRoles={[USER_ROLES.ADMIN, USER_ROLES.OPERATOR]}
                  />
                }
              >
                <Route path="/recovery" element={<Recovery />} />
              </Route>

              {/* Admin and Auditor */}
              <Route
                element={
                  <ProtectedRoute
                    allowedRoles={[USER_ROLES.ADMIN, USER_ROLES.AUDITOR]}
                  />
                }
              >
                <Route path="/audit" element={<Audit />} />
              </Route>
            </Route>
          </Routes>
        </BrowserRouter>
      </AuthProvider>
    </ThemeProvider>
  );
}

export default App;

  @media (max-width: 1024px) {
    font-size: 20px;
  }
}

  margin: 0;
}

.counter {
  font-family: var(--mono);

  border-radius: 4px;
  color: var(--text-h);
}

import { NavLink, Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

import {
  Sun,
  Moon,
  ShieldCheck,
  LogIn,
  LogOut,
  UserCircle,
} from "lucide-react";

import { useTheme } from "../context/ThemeContext";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";

import { NAVIGATION_ITEMS } from "../data/navigation";

import "../styles/navbar.css";

function Navbar() {
  const { isDark, toggleTheme } = useTheme();

  const { user, isAuthenticated, logout, hasRole } = useAuth();

  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const visibleNavigation = NAVIGATION_ITEMS.filter((item) => {
    if (item.public) {
      return true;
    }

    if (!isAuthenticated) {
      return false;
    }

    if (!item.roles) {
      return true;
    }

    return hasRole(item.roles);
  });

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();

    navigate("/", { replace: true });
  };

  return (
    <header className="navbar">
      <Link to="/" className="navbar-brand">
        <div className="brand-icon">
          <ShieldCheck size={23} />
        </div>

        <div className="brand-details">
          <span className="brand-name">EvidenceVault</span>

          <span className="brand-subtitle">SECURE EVIDENCE SYSTEM</span>
        </div>
      </Link>

      <nav className="navbar-links">
        {visibleNavigation.map((item) => {
          const Icon = item.icon;

          return (
            <NavLink
              key={item.path}
              to={item.path}
              end={item.path === "/"}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                `nav-link ${isActive ? "active" : ""}`
              }
            >
              <Icon size={17} />
              <span>{item.label}</span>
            </NavLink>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      <div className="navbar-actions">
        <button
          className="theme-toggle"
          onClick={toggleTheme}
          title={isDark ? "Switch to light mode" : "Switch to dark mode"}
          aria-label="Toggle theme"
        >
          {isDark ? <Sun size={19} /> : <Moon size={19} />}
        </button>

        {isAuthenticated ? (
          <div className="user-session">
            <div className="user-info">
              <UserCircle size={18} />

              <div className="user-details">
                <span>{user.username}</span>
                <small>{user.roles?.join(", ")}</small>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              className="logout-button"
              onClick={handleLogout}
              title="Logout"
            >
              <LogOut size={17} />
              <span>Logout</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <Link to="/login" className="login-button">
            <LogIn size={17} />
            <span>Sign In</span>
          </Link>
        )}
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

export default Navbar;

========================================
FILE: components\ProtectedRoute.jsx

import { Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";

import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";

function ProtectedRoute({ allowedRoles = [] }) {
  const { isAuthenticated, hasRole } = useAuth();

  const location = useLocation();

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace state={{ from: location }} />;
  }

  if (allowedRoles.length > 0 && !hasRole(allowedRoles)) {
    return <Navigate to="/unauthorized" replace />;
  }

  return <Outlet />;
}

export default ProtectedRoute;

function StatusBadge({ status }) {
  const normalizedStatus = String(status || "UNKNOWN").toUpperCase();

  const isHealthy = [
    "UP",
    "SUCCESS",
    "COMPLETED",
    "HEALTHY",
    "RUNNING",
  ].includes(normalizedStatus);

  const isWarning = ["NOT_CHECKED", "PENDING", "IN_PROGRESS"].includes(
    normalizedStatus,
  );

  const color = isHealthy ? "success" : isWarning ? "warning" : "danger";

  return (
    <span className={`status-badge ${color}`}>
      <span className="status-indicator"></span>
      {normalizedStatus.replaceAll("_", " ")}
    </span>
  );
}

export default StatusBadge;

FILE: context\AuthContext.jsx

import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from "react";

import {
  loginUser,
  saveAuthSession,
  getStoredUser,
  clearAuthSession,
} from "../services/authService";

const AuthContext = createContext(null);

export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(() => getStoredUser());

  const [isAuthenticated, setIsAuthenticated] = useState(() =>
    Boolean(localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-token") && getStoredUser()),
  );

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  // Restore the saved session on application startup.
  useEffect(() => {
    const storedUser = getStoredUser();
    const storedToken = localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-token");

    if (storedUser && storedToken) {
      setUser(storedUser);
      setIsAuthenticated(true);
    } else {
      clearAuthSession();
      setUser(null);
      setIsAuthenticated(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const login = async (credentials) => {
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const authData = await loginUser(credentials);

      if (!authData?.accessToken) {
        throw new Error("Authentication token was not received.");
      }

      saveAuthSession(authData);

      setUser({
        username: authData.username,
        roles: authData.roles || [],
        tokenType: authData.tokenType,
        expiresIn: authData.expiresIn,
      });

      setIsAuthenticated(true);

      return authData;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const logout = () => {
    clearAuthSession();

    setUser(null);
    setIsAuthenticated(false);
  };

  const hasRole = (allowedRoles = []) => {
    if (!user || !Array.isArray(user.roles)) {
      return false;
    }

    return allowedRoles.some((role) => user.roles.includes(role));
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        user,
        isAuthenticated,
        loading,
        login,
        logout,
        hasRole,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth() {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useAuth must be used inside AuthProvider");
  }

  return context;
}

========================================
FILE: context\ThemeContext.jsx
========================================
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from "react";

const ThemeContext = createContext(null);

export function ThemeProvider({ children }) {
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState(() => {
    return localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-theme") || "dark";
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", theme);

    localStorage.setItem("evidence-vault-theme", theme);
  }, [theme]);

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    setTheme((currentTheme) => (currentTheme === "dark" ? "light" : "dark"));
  };

  return (
    <ThemeContext.Provider
      value={{
        theme,
        toggleTheme,
        isDark: theme === "dark",
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </ThemeContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useTheme() {
  const context = useContext(ThemeContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useTheme must be used inside ThemeProvider");
  }

  return context;
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  Activity,
  RefreshCw,
  Search,

  Clock,

function Audit() {
  const [documents, setDocuments] = useState([]);
  const [selectedDocumentId, setSelectedDocumentId] = useState("");
  const [auditLogs, setAuditLogs] = useState([]);

  const [loadingDocuments, setLoadingDocuments] = useState(true);
  const [loadingAudit, setLoadingAudit] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState("");

  const loadDocuments = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoadingDocuments(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const response = await getDocuments({
        page: 0,
        size: 100,
      });

      const records = response?.content || [];
      setDocuments(records);

      if (records.length > 0) {
        setSelectedDocumentId(
          (currentId) => currentId || String(records[0].id),
        );
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Unable to load documents.");
    } finally {
      setLoadingDocuments(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const loadAuditHistory = useCallback(async (documentId) => {
    if (!documentId) {
      setAuditLogs([]);
      return;
    }

    setLoadingAudit(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const response = await getDocumentAuditHistory(documentId);
      setAuditLogs(Array.isArray(response) ? response : []);
    } catch (err) {
      setAuditLogs([]);
      setError(err.message || "Unable to load audit history.");
    } finally {
      setLoadingAudit(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadDocuments();
  }, [loadDocuments]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedDocumentId) {
      loadAuditHistory(selectedDocumentId);
    }
  }, [selectedDocumentId, loadAuditHistory]);

  const filteredLogs = useMemo(() => {
    const term = searchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!term) return auditLogs;

    return auditLogs.filter((log) =>
      [log.username, log.action, log.details, log.documentId]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" ")
        .toLowerCase()
        .includes(term),
    );
  }, [auditLogs, searchTerm]);

  const selectedDocument = documents.find(
    (document) => String(document.id) === selectedDocumentId,
  );

  const formatDate = (value) => {
    if (!value) return "Unknown date";

    const date = new Date(value);

    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return value;

    return date.toLocaleString();
  };

  const formatAction = (action) =>
    String(action || "UNKNOWN").replaceAll("_", " ");

  const getActionClass = (action) => {
    const value = String(action || "").toUpperCase();

    if (value.includes("DELETE")) return "audit-action-delete";
    if (value.includes("RESTORE")) return "audit-action-restore";
    if (value.includes("UPLOAD")) return "audit-action-upload";
    if (value.includes("DOWNLOAD")) return "audit-action-download";
    if (value.includes("PREVIEW")) return "audit-action-preview";

    return "audit-action-default";
  };

  return (
    <main className="audit-page">
      <section className="audit-header">
        <div>
          <div className="audit-title-row">
            <ShieldCheck size={28} />
            <h1>Audit Trail</h1>
          </div>

          <p>Track document activities, users, and recorded events.</p>
        </div>

        <button
          className="audit-refresh-button"
          onClick={() => {
            if (selectedDocumentId) {
              loadAuditHistory(selectedDocumentId);
            } else {
              loadDocuments();
            }
          }}
          disabled={loadingAudit || loadingDocuments}
        >
          <RefreshCw size={16} />
          Refresh
        </button>
      </section>

      <section className="audit-controls">
        <div className="audit-document-selector">
          <label htmlFor="audit-document">Select Document</label>

          <select
            id="audit-document"
            value={selectedDocumentId}
            onChange={(event) => setSelectedDocumentId(event.target.value)}
            disabled={loadingDocuments || documents.length === 0}
          >
            {documents.length === 0 && (
              <option value="">No documents available</option>
            )}

            {documents.map((document) => (
              <option key={document.id} value={String(document.id)}>
                #{document.id} â€” {document.originalFilename}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        <div className="audit-search">
          <Search size={17} />

          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Search user, action or details..."
            value={searchTerm}
            onChange={(event) => setSearchTerm(event.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
      </section>

      {selectedDocument && (
        <section className="audit-document-card">
          <div className="audit-document-icon">
            <FileText size={22} />
          </div>

          <div>
            <h3>{selectedDocument.originalFilename}</h3>
            <p>
              Document ID: #{selectedDocument.id}
              {" Â· "}
              Category: {selectedDocument.category}
              {" Â· "}
              Status: {selectedDocument.status}
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="audit-error">
          <AlertCircle size={18} />
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <section className="audit-summary">
        <div className="audit-summary-icon">
          <Activity size={21} />
        </div>

        <div>
          <span>Total Recorded Events</span>
          <strong>{auditLogs.length}</strong>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="audit-history-section">
        <div className="audit-section-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Activity History</h2>
            <p>Events are displayed in newest-first order.</p>
          </div>

          <span className="audit-event-count">
            {filteredLogs.length} events
          </span>
        </div>

        {loadingDocuments || loadingAudit ? (
          <div className="audit-state">
            <RefreshCw className="audit-spin" size={24} />
            <p>Loading audit history...</p>
          </div>
        ) : filteredLogs.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="audit-state">
            <FileText size={32} />
            <h3>No audit events found</h3>
            <p>
              {auditLogs.length === 0
                ? "No recorded activities are available for this document."
                : "Try a different search term."}
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="audit-table-wrapper">
            <table className="audit-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Event ID</th>
                  <th>Action</th>
                  <th>User</th>
                  <th>Details</th>
                  <th>Timestamp</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>

              <tbody>
                {filteredLogs.map((log) => (
                  <tr key={log.id}>
                    <td>#{log.id}</td>

                    <td>
                      <span
                        className={`audit-action ${getActionClass(log.action)}`}
                      >
                        {formatAction(log.action)}
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    <td>
                      <span className="audit-user">
                        <User size={15} />
                        {log.username}
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    <td>{log.details || "â€”"}</td>

                    <td>
                      <span className="audit-time">
                        <Clock size={14} />
                        {formatDate(log.createdAt)}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

export default Audit;

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";

import {
  Activity,
  AlertTriangle,
  ArrowDownToLine,
  ArrowRight,
  ArrowUpRight,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock3,
  Cloud,
  Database,
  FileCheck2,
  FileClock,
  FileText,
  Files,
  HardDrive,
  RefreshCw,
  ShieldAlert,
  ShieldCheck,
  Trash2,
  Users,
  XCircle,
} from "lucide-react";

import {
  Area,
  AreaChart,
  CartesianGrid,
  Cell,
  Legend,
  Pie,
  PieChart,
  ResponsiveContainer,
  Tooltip,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
} from "recharts";

import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { USER_ROLES } from "../config/constants";

import { getDashboardData } from "../services/dashboardService";

import "../styles/dashboard.css";

const CHART_COLORS = ["#22c55e", "#ef4444", "#f59e0b"];

function toArray(data) {
  if (Array.isArray(data)) return data;

  if (Array.isArray(data?.content)) return data.content;
  if (Array.isArray(data?.items)) return data.items;
  if (Array.isArray(data?.results)) return data.results;

  return [];
}

function formatNumber(value) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return "â€”";

  return Number(value).toLocaleString();
}

function formatBytes(bytes) {
  const value = Number(bytes);

  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) return "â€”";
  if (value === 0) return "0 Bytes";

  const units = ["Bytes", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"];
  const index = Math.min(
    Math.floor(Math.log(value) / Math.log(1024)),
    units.length - 1,
  );

  return `${(value / Math.pow(1024, index)).toFixed(1)} ${units[index]}`;
}

function formatSeconds(value) {
  if (
    value === null ||
    value === undefined ||
    value === "" ||
    !Number.isFinite(Number(value))
  ) {
    return "â€”";
  }

  return `${Number(value).toFixed(3)} sec`;
}

function formatDate(value) {
  if (!value) return "Not recorded";

  const date = new Date(value);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "Not recorded";

  return date.toLocaleString(undefined, {
    dateStyle: "medium",
    timeStyle: "short",
  });
}

function getLatestRecord(records) {
  if (!records.length) return null;

  return [...records].sort((a, b) => {
    return new Date(b.created_at) - new Date(a.created_at);
  })[0];
}

function normalizeStatus(value) {
  return String(value || "UNKNOWN").toUpperCase();
}

function getStatusTone(value) {
  const status = normalizeStatus(value);

  if (["UP", "SUCCESS", "COMPLETED", "VERIFIED", "ACTIVE"].includes(status)) {
    return "success";
  }

  if (["FAILED", "DOWN", "ERROR", "CORRUPTED", "MISMATCH"].includes(status)) {
    return "danger";
  }

  if (
    ["PENDING", "WARNING", "RECOVERY_REQUIRED", "IN_PROGRESS"].includes(status)
  ) {
    return "warning";
  }

  return "neutral";
}

function StatusPill({ status, label }) {
  const tone = getStatusTone(status);

  return (
    <span className={`dash-status dash-status-${tone}`}>
      <span className="dash-status-dot" />
      {label || normalizeStatus(status).replaceAll("_", " ")}
    </span>
  );
}

function SectionHeading({ eyebrow, title, description, action }) {
  return (
    <div className="dash-section-heading">
      <div>
        {eyebrow && <span className="dash-eyebrow">{eyebrow}</span>}
        <h2>{title}</h2>
        {description && <p>{description}</p>}
      </div>

      {action}
    </div>
  );
}

function MetricCard({
  title,
  value,
  description,
  icon: Icon,
  tone = "blue",
  to,
}) {
  const content = (
    <>
      <div className={`dash-metric-icon tone-${tone}`}>
        <Icon size={20} />
      </div>

      <div className="dash-metric-content">
        <span className="dash-metric-title">{title}</span>
        <strong>{value}</strong>
        <small>{description}</small>
      </div>

      {to && <ArrowUpRight className="dash-metric-arrow" size={17} />}
    </>
  );

  if (to) {
    return (
      <Link to={to} className="dash-metric-card">
        {content}
      </Link>
    );
  }

  return <article className="dash-metric-card">{content}</article>;
}

function EmptyState({ icon: Icon = Files, title, description }) {
  return (
    <div className="dash-empty">
      <Icon size={27} />
      <strong>{title}</strong>
      <span>{description}</span>
    </div>
  );
}

function RegionCard({
  region,
  code,
  name,
  verification,
  verifiedAt,
  secondary = false,
}) {
  const hasResult = verification === true || verification === false;

  return (
    <article className="dash-region-card">
      <div className="dash-region-top">
        <div className={`dash-region-flag ${secondary ? "singapore" : ""}`}>
          {region}
        </div>

        <div className="dash-region-info">
          <strong>{name}</strong>
          <span>{code}</span>
        </div>

        <span className="dash-region-type">
          {secondary ? "REPLICA" : "PRIMARY"}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="dash-region-bottom">
        <div>
          <span>Last recorded verification</span>
          <strong>
            {hasResult
              ? verification
                ? "Checksum verified"
                : "Verification failed"
              : "No verification recorded"}
          </strong>
        </div>

        <StatusPill
          status={
            hasResult ? (verification ? "VERIFIED" : "FAILED") : "UNKNOWN"
          }
          label={
            hasResult ? (verification ? "Verified" : "Failed") : "Not checked"
          }
        />
      </div>

      <small className="dash-region-date">
        {verifiedAt ? formatDate(verifiedAt) : "Awaiting first recorded drill"}
      </small>
    </article>
  );
}

function Dashboard() {
  const { hasRole, user } = useAuth();

  const canViewRecovery = hasRole([USER_ROLES.ADMIN, USER_ROLES.AUDITOR]);

  const [dashboard, setDashboard] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [pageError, setPageError] = useState("");
  const [lastUpdated, setLastUpdated] = useState(null);

  const loadDashboard = useCallback(
    async (isRefresh = false) => {
      if (isRefresh) {
        setRefreshing(true);
      } else {
        setLoading(true);
      }

      setPageError("");

      try {
        const result = await getDashboardData({
          includeRecoveryData: canViewRecovery,
        });

        setDashboard(result);
        setLastUpdated(new Date());
      } catch (error) {
        setPageError(error?.message || "Unable to load dashboard information.");
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
        setRefreshing(false);
      }
    },
    [canViewRecovery],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    loadDashboard();
  }, [loadDashboard]);

  const documents = dashboard?.documents || {
    total: 0,
    active: 0,
    deleted: 0,
    corrupted: 0,
    recent: [],
  };

  const metrics = useMemo(() => toArray(dashboard?.metrics), [dashboard]);

  const drills = useMemo(() => toArray(dashboard?.drills), [dashboard]);

  const manifests = useMemo(() => toArray(dashboard?.manifests), [dashboard]);

  const recentDocuments = useMemo(() => {
    return [...toArray(documents.recent)].sort(
      (a, b) => new Date(b.createdAt) - new Date(a.createdAt),
    );
  }, [documents.recent]);

  const latestDrill = useMemo(() => getLatestRecord(drills), [drills]);

  const latestManifest = useMemo(() => getLatestRecord(manifests), [manifests]);

  const drillSummary = useMemo(() => {
    const successful = drills.filter((item) =>
      ["SUCCESS", "COMPLETED"].includes(normalizeStatus(item.drill_status)),
    ).length;

    const failed = drills.filter(
      (item) => normalizeStatus(item.drill_status) === "FAILED",
    ).length;

    const attention = drills.filter((item) =>
      ["RECOVERY_REQUIRED", "WARNING"].includes(
        normalizeStatus(item.drill_status),
      ),
    ).length;

    return {
      total: drills.length,
      successful,
      failed,
      attention,
      successRate: drills.length
        ? Number(((successful / drills.length) * 100).toFixed(1))
        : null,
      recoveryOperations: drills.filter(
        (item) => item.recovery_performed === true,
      ).length,
    };
  }, [drills]);

  const metricSummary = useMemo(() => {
    const rtoValues = metrics
      .map((item) => Number(item.recovery_workflow_seconds))
      .filter(Number.isFinite);

    const rpoValues = metrics
      .map((item) => Number(item.estimated_rpo_seconds))
      .filter(Number.isFinite);

    const average = (values) =>
      values.length
        ? values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / values.length
        : null;

    return {
      averageRto: average(rtoValues),
      averageRpo: average(rpoValues),
      replicationCompleted: metrics.filter(
        (item) => item.replication_status === "COMPLETED",
      ).length,
      replicationFailed: metrics.filter(
        (item) => item.replication_status === "FAILED",
      ).length,
      replicationObserved: metrics.filter(
        (item) => item.replication_status !== "NOT_AVAILABLE",
      ).length,
    };
  }, [metrics]);

  const rtoChartData = useMemo(() => {
    return [...metrics]
      .filter((item) => Number.isFinite(Number(item.recovery_workflow_seconds)))
      .sort((a, b) => new Date(a.created_at) - new Date(b.created_at))
      .slice(-10)
      .map((item, index) => ({
        name: `Drill ${index + 1}`,
        rto: Number(item.recovery_workflow_seconds),
        date: formatDate(item.created_at),
      }));
  }, [metrics]);

  const drillChartData = useMemo(() => {
    return [
      { name: "Successful", value: drillSummary.successful },
      { name: "Failed", value: drillSummary.failed },
      { name: "Attention", value: drillSummary.attention },
    ].filter((item) => item.value > 0);
  }, [drillSummary]);

  const system = dashboard?.system;

  const systemHealthy =
    system?.application === "UP" && system?.database === "UP";

  const latestDrillDate = latestDrill?.created_at;

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <main className="dashboard-page">
        <div className="dash-loading">
          <RefreshCw className="spin" size={25} />
          <strong>Preparing your EvidenceVault overview</strong>
          <span>
            Collecting document, infrastructure and recovery information.
          </span>
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  if (pageError && !dashboard) {
    return (
      <main className="dashboard-page">
        <div className="dash-error">
          <AlertTriangle size={30} />
          <h2>Dashboard unavailable</h2>
          <p>{pageError}</p>
          <button onClick={() => loadDashboard()}>
            <RefreshCw size={16} />
            Try again
          </button>
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="dashboard-page">
      {/* PAGE HEADER */}
      <header className="dash-page-header">
        <div>
          <span className="dash-eyebrow">EVIDENCE VAULT / OVERVIEW</span>
          <h1>Good to see you, {user?.username || "User"}</h1>
          <p>
            Your document protection, infrastructure and recovery operations at
            a glance.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="dash-header-actions">
          <div className="dash-updated">
            <span>Last updated</span>
            <strong>
              {lastUpdated
                ? lastUpdated.toLocaleTimeString([], {
                    hour: "2-digit",
                    minute: "2-digit",
                  })
                : "â€”"}
            </strong>
          </div>

          <button
            className="dash-refresh-button"
            onClick={() => loadDashboard(true)}
            disabled={refreshing}
          >
            <RefreshCw size={16} className={refreshing ? "spin" : ""} />
            {refreshing ? "Refreshing" : "Refresh"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      {pageError && (
        <div className="dash-alert dash-alert-warning">
          <AlertTriangle size={17} />
          Some information could not be refreshed. Check the affected sections
          below.
        </div>
      )}

      {/* SYSTEM STATUS STRIP */}
      <section className="dash-system-banner">
        <div className="dash-system-banner-main">
          <span
            className={`dash-system-pulse ${systemHealthy ? "healthy" : "unhealthy"}`}
          />
          <div>
            <strong>
              {systemHealthy
                ? "Core application services are responding"
                : "Core service status needs attention"}
            </strong>
            <span>
              Application and database connectivity reported by the backend
              status endpoint.
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="dash-system-banner-status">
          <StatusPill
            status={system?.application || "UNKNOWN"}
            label={`Application ${system?.application || "Unknown"}`}
          />
          <StatusPill
            status={system?.database || "UNKNOWN"}
            label={`Database ${system?.database || "Unknown"}`}
          />
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* DOCUMENT KPI CARDS */}
      <section className="dash-section">
        <SectionHeading
          eyebrow="DOCUMENT LIFECYCLE"
          title="Document overview"
          description="Understand how many documents are stored and their current lifecycle state."
          action={
            <Link to="/documents" className="dash-text-link">
              Manage documents <ArrowRight size={15} />
            </Link>
          }
        />

        <div className="dash-metric-grid">
          <MetricCard
            title="Total documents"
            value={formatNumber(documents.total)}
            description="All document records"
            icon={Files}
            tone="blue"
            to="/documents"
          />

          <MetricCard
            title="Active documents"
            value={formatNumber(documents.active)}
            description="Available in the vault"
            icon={FileCheck2}
            tone="green"
            to="/documents"
          />

          <MetricCard
            title="Recently deleted"
            value={formatNumber(documents.deleted)}
            description="Soft-deleted records"
            icon={Trash2}
            tone="orange"
            to="/documents"
          />

          <MetricCard
            title="Corrupted documents"
            value={formatNumber(documents.corrupted)}
            description="Integrity attention required"
            icon={ShieldAlert}
            tone="red"
            to="/documents"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="dash-document-summary">
          <div className="dash-summary-label">
            <span>Active document ratio</span>
            <strong>
              {documents.total
                ? `${((documents.active / documents.total) * 100).toFixed(1)}%`
                : "â€”"}
            </strong>
          </div>

          <div className="dash-progress-track">
            <div
              className="dash-progress-active"
              style={{
                width: documents.total
                  ? `${Math.min((documents.active / documents.total) * 100, 100)}%`
                  : "0%",
              }}
            />
          </div>

          <span>
            {formatNumber(documents.active)} active out of{" "}
            {formatNumber(documents.total)} total records
          </span>
        </div>

        {dashboard?.errors?.documents && (
          <p className="dash-section-error">
            Document information could not be loaded:{" "}
            {dashboard.errors.documents}
          </p>
        )}
      </section>

      {/* RECOVERY KPI CARDS */}
      <section className="dash-section">
        <SectionHeading
          eyebrow="BUSINESS CONTINUITY"
          title="Disaster recovery performance"
          description="Measured results from previously recorded recovery operations."
          action={
            canViewRecovery ? (
              <Link to="/recovery" className="dash-text-link">
                Open recovery center <ArrowRight size={15} />
              </Link>
            ) : null
          }
        />

        {canViewRecovery ? (
          <>
            <div className="dash-metric-grid dash-recovery-grid">
              <MetricCard
                title="Total DR drills"
                value={formatNumber(drillSummary.total)}
                description="Recorded operations"
                icon={Activity}
                tone="blue"
              />

              <MetricCard
                title="Drill success rate"
                value={
                  drillSummary.successRate === null
                    ? "â€”"
                    : `${drillSummary.successRate}%`
                }
                description={`${drillSummary.successful} successful`}
                icon={CheckCircle2}
                tone="green"
              />

              <MetricCard
                title="Average recovery time"
                value={formatSeconds(metricSummary.averageRto)}
                description="Measured recovery workflow"
                icon={Clock3}
                tone="purple"
              />

              <MetricCard
                title="Estimated RPO"
                value={formatSeconds(metricSummary.averageRpo)}
                description="Average timestamp-based estimate"
                icon={ArrowDownToLine}
                tone="orange"
              />

              <MetricCard
                title="Recovery operations"
                value={formatNumber(drillSummary.recoveryOperations)}
                description="Drills where recovery was performed"
                icon={RefreshCw}
                tone="blue"
              />

              <MetricCard
                title="Recovery manifests"
                value={formatNumber(manifests.length)}
                description="Recorded recovery evidence"
                icon={FileCheck2}
                tone="green"
              />
            </div>

            {dashboard?.errors?.metrics && (
              <p className="dash-section-error">
                Recovery metrics unavailable: {dashboard.errors.metrics}
              </p>
            )}

            {dashboard?.errors?.drills && (
              <p className="dash-section-error">
                Drill history unavailable: {dashboard.errors.drills}
              </p>
            )}
          </>
        ) : (
          <div className="dash-permission-note">
            <ShieldCheck size={21} />
            <div>
              <strong>Recovery analytics are role-restricted</strong>
              <span>
                Detailed drill history, RTO and recovery evidence are available
                to Admin and Auditor accounts.
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      {/* ANALYTICS AND SYSTEM HEALTH */}
      <section className="dash-content-grid">
        <div className="dash-panel">
          <SectionHeading
            eyebrow="PERFORMANCE"
            title="Recovery time trend"
            description="Recovery workflow duration from recorded metrics."
          />

          {canViewRecovery && rtoChartData.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="dash-chart">
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={260}>
                <AreaChart data={rtoChartData}>
                  <defs>
                    <linearGradient id="rtoFill" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
                      <stop
                        offset="0%"
                        stopColor="#6385f5"
                        stopOpacity={0.35}
                      />
                      <stop
                        offset="95%"
                        stopColor="#6385f5"
                        stopOpacity={0.02}
                      />
                    </linearGradient>
                  </defs>

                  <CartesianGrid
                    stroke="var(--border-color)"
                    strokeDasharray="4 4"
                    vertical={false}
                  />

                  <XAxis
                    dataKey="name"
                    tick={{ fill: "var(--text-muted)", fontSize: 11 }}
                    axisLine={false}
                    tickLine={false}
                  />

                  <YAxis
                    tick={{ fill: "var(--text-muted)", fontSize: 11 }}
                    axisLine={false}
                    tickLine={false}
                    width={42}
                  />

                  <Tooltip
                    contentStyle={{
                      background: "var(--bg-card)",
                      border: "1px solid var(--border-color)",
                      borderRadius: 10,
                      color: "var(--text-primary)",
                    }}
                    formatter={(value) => [
                      `${Number(value).toFixed(3)} sec`,
                      "Recovery time",
                    ]}
                    labelFormatter={(label, payload) =>
                      payload?.[0]?.payload?.date || label
                    }
                  />

                  <Area
                    type="monotone"
                    dataKey="rto"
                    stroke="#6385f5"
                    strokeWidth={2.5}
                    fill="url(#rtoFill)"
                    activeDot={{ r: 5 }}
                  />
                </AreaChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <EmptyState
              icon={Clock3}
              title="No recovery measurements yet"
              description={
                canViewRecovery
                  ? "Run a recovery drill to start building your RTO history."
                  : "Recovery measurements are restricted to Admin and Auditor roles."
              }
            />
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="dash-panel">
          <SectionHeading
            eyebrow="OUTCOMES"
            title="Drill results"
            description="Distribution of recorded recovery drill outcomes."
          />

          {canViewRecovery && drillChartData.length > 0 ? (
            <>
              <div className="dash-chart dash-pie-chart">
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={220}>
                  <PieChart>
                    <Pie
                      data={drillChartData}
                      dataKey="value"
                      nameKey="name"
                      innerRadius={57}
                      outerRadius={83}

                        />
                      ))}
                    </Pie>

                    <Tooltip
                      contentStyle={{
                        background: "var(--bg-card)",

                        color: "var(--text-primary)",
                      }}
                    />

                    <Legend
                      verticalAlign="bottom"
                      iconType="circle"
                      formatter={(value) => (
                        <span className="dash-chart-legend">{value}</span>
                      )}
                    />
                  </PieChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </div>

              <div className="dash-outcome-summary">

                  <span>Successful</span>
                  <strong className="text-success">
                    {drillSummary.successful}
                  </strong>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>Failed</span>
                  <strong className="text-danger">{drillSummary.failed}</strong>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>Attention</span>

          ) : (

              description={
                canViewRecovery
                  ? "Recovery drill results will appear here."
