          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* INFRASTRUCTURE */}
      <section className="dash-section">
        <SectionHeading
          eyebrow="INFRASTRUCTURE"

          description="Regional verification results from the latest recorded disaster recovery drill."
        />

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";

import {
  Search,
  Upload,
  Download,
  Eye,
  Trash2,
  RotateCcw,
  FileText,
  FileImage,
  File,
  RefreshCw,
  X,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  FolderOpen,
  AlertCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
} from "lucide-react";

import {
  getDocuments,
  uploadDocument,
  deleteDocument,
  restoreDocument,
  downloadDocument,
} from "../services/documentService";

import {
  formatFileSize,
  formatDocumentDate,
  getFileExtension,
  isPreviewable,
  getDocumentStatus,
} from "../services/documentUtils";

import "../styles/documents.css";

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

const EMPTY_FILTERS = {
  keyword: "",
  category: "",
  status: "",
};

// --------------------------------------------------
// Document icon
// --------------------------------------------------

function DocumentIcon({ contentType }) {
  if (contentType?.startsWith("image/")) {
    return <FileImage size={20} />;
  }

  if (contentType === "application/pdf") {
    return <FileText size={20} />;
  }

  return <File size={20} />;
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// Document status
// --------------------------------------------------

function DocumentStatus({ status }) {
  const value = getDocumentStatus(status);

  const className =
    value === "ACTIVE" || value === "AVAILABLE"
      ? "document-status-active"
      : value === "DELETED"
        ? "document-status-deleted"
        : "document-status-neutral";

  return (
    <span className={`document-status ${className}`}>
      {value.replaceAll("_", " ")}
    </span>
  );
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// Upload modal
// --------------------------------------------------

function UploadModal({ onClose, onUpload, uploading }) {
  const [file, setFile] = useState(null);
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState("");
  const [validationError, setValidationError] = useState("");

  function handleSubmit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();

    if (!file) {
      setValidationError("Please select a document.");
      return;
    }

    if (!category.trim()) {
      setValidationError("Please enter a document category.");
      return;
    }

    setValidationError("");

    onUpload(file, category.trim(), description.trim());
  }

  return (
    <div
      className="document-modal-overlay"
      onClick={uploading ? undefined : onClose}
    >
      <section
        className="document-modal"
        onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="upload-modal-title"
      >
        <div className="modal-heading">
          <div>
            <h2 id="upload-modal-title">Upload document</h2>
            <p>Add a new document to your evidence vault.</p>
          </div>

          <button
            className="icon-button"
            onClick={onClose}
            type="button"
            disabled={uploading}
            aria-label="Close upload modal"
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <label className="document-form-label">Select document</label>

          <label className="file-drop-area">
            <Upload size={28} />

            <strong>{file ? file.name : "Choose a file to upload"}</strong>

            <span>
              {file
                ? formatFileSize(file.size)
                : "Select a file from your computer"}
            </span>

            <input
              type="file"
              onChange={(event) => {
                setFile(event.target.files?.[0] || null);
                setValidationError("");
              }}
              disabled={uploading}
            />
          </label>

          <label className="document-form-label">
            Category <span>*</span>
          </label>

          <input
            className="document-input"
            type="text"
            placeholder="e.g. Legal, Financial, Reports"
            value={category}
            onChange={(event) => setCategory(event.target.value)}
            required
            disabled={uploading}
          />

          <label className="document-form-label">Description</label>

          <textarea
            className="document-input document-textarea"
            placeholder="Enter a short description..."
            value={description}
            onChange={(event) => setDescription(event.target.value)}
            rows={3}
            disabled={uploading}
          />

          {validationError && (
            <p className="document-validation-error">{validationError}</p>
          )}

          <div className="modal-actions">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="secondary-button"
              onClick={onClose}
              disabled={uploading}
            >
              Cancel
            </button>

            <button
              type="submit"
              className="document-primary-button"
              disabled={uploading}
            >
              <Upload size={16} />
              {uploading ? "Uploading..." : "Upload document"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// Preview modal
// --------------------------------------------------

function PreviewModal({ document, previewUrl, previewText, onClose }) {
  const contentType = document.contentType?.toLowerCase() || "";

  const isImage = contentType.startsWith("image/");
  const isText =
    contentType.startsWith("text/") ||
    contentType === "application/json" ||
    contentType === "application/xml";

  return (
    <div className="document-modal-overlay" onClick={onClose}>
      <section
        className="document-preview-modal"
        onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="preview-modal-title"
      >
        <div className="modal-heading">
          <div>
            <h2 id="preview-modal-title">{document.originalFilename}</h2>

            <p>Document preview</p>
          </div>

          <button
            className="icon-button"
            onClick={onClose}
            type="button"
            aria-label="Close preview"
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {isImage ? (
          <div className="image-preview-area">
            <img src={previewUrl} alt={document.originalFilename} />
          </div>
        ) : isText ? (
          <div className="text-preview-area">
            <pre>{previewText}</pre>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <iframe
            className="pdf-preview-frame"
            src={previewUrl}
            title={document.originalFilename}
          />
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// Main Documents component
// --------------------------------------------------

export default function Documents() {
  // Document data
  const [documents, setDocuments] = useState([]);
  const [page, setPage] = useState(0);
  const [totalPages, setTotalPages] = useState(0);
  const [totalElements, setTotalElements] = useState(0);

  // Draft filters: values currently entered by the user
  const [keyword, setKeyword] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");

  // Applied filters: values actually sent to the backend
  const [appliedFilters, setAppliedFilters] = useState({
    ...EMPTY_FILTERS,
  });

  // Loading and action states
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [actionId, setActionId] = useState(null);

  // Modal states
  const [showUpload, setShowUpload] = useState(false);

  const [selectedPreviewDocument, setSelectedPreviewDocument] = useState(null);

  const [previewUrl, setPreviewUrl] = useState("");
  const [previewText, setPreviewText] = useState("");

  // Alert states
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  // Tracks the latest list request.
  // This prevents an older response from replacing newer data.
  const requestSequence = useRef(0);

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // Load documents
  // --------------------------------------------------

  const loadDocuments = useCallback(
    async (overrides = {}) => {
      const requestId = ++requestSequence.current;

      const requestPage = overrides.page ?? page;

      const requestFilters = overrides.filters ?? appliedFilters;

      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      try {
        const result = await getDocuments({
          keyword: requestFilters.keyword,
          category: requestFilters.category,
          status: requestFilters.status,
          page: requestPage,
          size: PAGE_SIZE,
        });

        // Ignore an outdated response.
        if (requestId !== requestSequence.current) {
          return;
        }

        setDocuments(Array.isArray(result?.content) ? result.content : []);

        setTotalPages(result?.totalPages ?? 0);
        setTotalElements(result?.totalElements ?? 0);
      } catch (err) {
        if (requestId !== requestSequence.current) {
          return;
        }

        setError(err?.message || "Unable to load documents.");

        setDocuments([]);
        setTotalPages(0);
        setTotalElements(0);
      } finally {
        if (requestId === requestSequence.current) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    },
    [page, appliedFilters],
  );

  // Automatically load when the page or applied filters change.
  useEffect(() => {
    loadDocuments();
  }, [loadDocuments]);

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // Preview URL cleanup
  // --------------------------------------------------

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (previewUrl) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(previewUrl);
      }
    };
  }, [previewUrl]);

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // Active documents summary
  // --------------------------------------------------

  const activeDocuments = useMemo(() => {
    return documents.filter(
      (item) => getDocumentStatus(item.status) !== "DELETED",
    );
  }, [documents]);

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // Upload document
  // --------------------------------------------------

  async function handleUpload(file, selectedCategory, description) {
    setUploading(true);
    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    try {
      await uploadDocument(file, selectedCategory, description);

      setShowUpload(false);

      // Reset the filters after a successful upload.
      setKeyword("");
      setCategory("");
      setStatus("");

      setAppliedFilters({
        ...EMPTY_FILTERS,
      });

      setPage(0);

      setMessage("Document uploaded successfully.");

      // Explicitly refresh page 0 with empty filters.
      await loadDocuments({
        page: 0,
        filters: EMPTY_FILTERS,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || "Document upload failed.");
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // Download document
  // --------------------------------------------------

  async function handleDownload(document) {
    setActionId(document.id);
    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    let downloadUrl = "";

    try {
      const blob = await downloadDocument(document.id);

      if (!(blob instanceof Blob) || blob.size === 0) {
        throw new Error("The downloaded document is empty.");
      }

      downloadUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

      const link = window.document.createElement("a");

      link.href = downloadUrl;
      link.download = document.originalFilename || "document";

      window.document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      link.remove();

      setMessage("Document download started.");
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || "Download failed.");
    } finally {
      if (downloadUrl) {
        window.setTimeout(() => {
          URL.revokeObjectURL(downloadUrl);
        }, 1000);
      }

      setActionId(null);
    }
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // Preview document
  // --------------------------------------------------

  async function handlePreview(document) {
    if (!isPreviewable(document.contentType, document.originalFilename)) {
      setError(
        "Preview is not supported for this file type. You can download the document instead.",
      );

      return;
    }

    setActionId(document.id);
    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    try {
      const blob = await downloadDocument(document.id);

      if (!(blob instanceof Blob) || blob.size === 0) {
        throw new Error("The preview response is empty.");
      }

      const contentType = document.contentType?.toLowerCase() || "";

      const isText =
        contentType.startsWith("text/") ||
        contentType === "application/json" ||
        contentType === "application/xml";

      // Text and CSV files are displayed as text.
      if (isText) {
        const text = await blob.text();

        setPreviewUrl("");
        setPreviewText(text);
        setSelectedPreviewDocument(document);
      } else {
        // PDF and image files use a temporary object URL.
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

        setPreviewText("");
        setPreviewUrl(url);
        setSelectedPreviewDocument(document);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || "Unable to preview document.");
    } finally {
      setActionId(null);
    }
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // Close preview
  // --------------------------------------------------

  function closePreview() {
    setSelectedPreviewDocument(null);
    setPreviewUrl("");
    setPreviewText("");
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // Delete document
  // --------------------------------------------------

  async function handleDelete(document) {
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      `Are you sure you want to delete "${document.originalFilename}"?`,
    );

    if (!confirmed) {
      return;
    }

    setActionId(document.id);
    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    try {
      await deleteDocument(document.id);

      setMessage("Document deleted successfully.");

      // If the deleted document was the only record
      // on the current page, move to the previous page.
      if (documents.length === 1 && page > 0) {
        setPage((current) => current - 1);
      } else {
        await loadDocuments();
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || "Unable to delete document.");
    } finally {
      setActionId(null);
    }
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // Restore document
  // --------------------------------------------------

  async function handleRestore(document) {
    setActionId(document.id);
    setError("");
    setMessage("");

    try {
      await restoreDocument(document.id);

      setMessage("Document restored successfully.");

      await loadDocuments();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err?.message || "Unable to restore document.");
    } finally {
      setActionId(null);
    }
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // Search and filters
  // --------------------------------------------------

  function applySearch(event) {
    event.preventDefault();

    setAppliedFilters({
      keyword: keyword.trim(),
      category: category.trim(),
      status,
    });

    setPage(0);
  }

  function clearFilters() {
    setKeyword("");
    setCategory("");
    setStatus("");

    setAppliedFilters({
      ...EMPTY_FILTERS,
    });

    setPage(0);
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // Refresh
  // --------------------------------------------------

  function handleRefresh() {
    loadDocuments();
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // Render
  // --------------------------------------------------

  return (
    <main className="documents-page">
      {/* Page heading */}

      <section className="documents-heading">
        <div>
          <span className="documents-eyebrow">EVIDENCE VAULT</span>

          <h1>Document Management</h1>

          <p>Upload, organize, preview and manage your protected documents.</p>
        </div>

        <button
          className="document-primary-button"
          onClick={() => setShowUpload(true)}
          type="button"
        >
          <Upload size={17} />
          Upload document
        </button>
      </section>

      {/* Summary cards */}

      <section className="document-summary-grid">
        <div className="document-summary-card">
          <div className="document-summary-icon">
            <FolderOpen size={21} />
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Total records</span>
            <strong>{totalElements}</strong>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="document-summary-card">
          <div className="document-summary-icon summary-green">
            <CheckCircle2 size={21} />
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Active on this page</span>
            <strong>{activeDocuments.length}</strong>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="document-summary-card">
          <div className="document-summary-icon summary-purple">
            <FileText size={21} />
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Current page</span>

            <strong>
              {totalPages > 0 ? page + 1 : 0} / {totalPages}
            </strong>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Success message */}

      {message && (
        <div className="document-alert document-alert-success">
          <CheckCircle2 size={17} />

          <span>{message}</span>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMessage("")}
            aria-label="Dismiss success message"
          >
            <X size={15} />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Error message */}

      {error && (
        <div className="document-alert document-alert-error">
          <AlertCircle size={17} />

          <span>{error}</span>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setError("")}
            aria-label="Dismiss error message"
          >
            <X size={15} />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Search and filters */}

      <section className="document-toolbar">
        <form className="document-search-form" onSubmit={applySearch}>
          <div className="document-search-box">
            <Search size={18} />

            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Search document name..."
              value={keyword}
              onChange={(event) => setKeyword(event.target.value)}
            />
          </div>

          <input
            className="document-filter-input"
            type="text"
            placeholder="Category"
            value={category}
            onChange={(event) => setCategory(event.target.value)}
          />

          <select
            className="document-filter-input"
            value={status}
            onChange={(event) => setStatus(event.target.value)}
          >
            <option value="">All statuses</option>
            <option value="ACTIVE">Active</option>
            <option value="DELETED">Deleted</option>
          </select>

          <button
            type="submit"
            className="document-search-button"
            disabled={loading}
          >
            Search
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            className="document-clear-button"
            onClick={clearFilters}
          >
            Clear
          </button>
        </form>

        <button
          type="button"
          className="document-refresh-button"
          onClick={handleRefresh}
          disabled={loading}
          title="Refresh documents"
          aria-label="Refresh documents"
        >
          <RefreshCw size={17} className={loading ? "documents-spin" : ""} />
        </button>
      </section>

      {/* Documents table */}

      <section className="documents-table-card">
        <div className="documents-table-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Your documents</h2>

            <p>
              Showing {documents.length} of {totalElements} records
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Loading state */}

        {loading ? (
          <div className="documents-empty-state">
            <RefreshCw className="documents-spin" size={25} />

            <p>Loading documents...</p>
          </div>
        ) : documents.length === 0 ? (
          /* Empty state */

          <div className="documents-empty-state">
            <FolderOpen size={35} />

            <h3>No documents found</h3>

            <p>Try changing your search filters or upload a new document.</p>

            <button
              type="button"
              className="document-primary-button"
              onClick={() => setShowUpload(true)}
            >
              <Upload size={16} />
              Upload document
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* Table */

          <div className="documents-table-scroll">
            <table className="documents-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Document</th>
                  <th>Category</th>
                  <th>Size</th>
                  <th>Checksum</th>
                  <th>Status</th>
                  <th>Uploaded by</th>
                  <th>Upload date</th>
                  <th>Actions</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>

              <tbody>
                {documents.map((document) => {
                  const deleted =
                    getDocumentStatus(document.status) === "DELETED";

                  const rowLoading = actionId === document.id;

                  return (
                    <tr key={document.id}>
                      {/* Document name */}

                      <td>
                        <div className="document-name-cell">
                          <div className="document-file-icon">
                            <DocumentIcon contentType={document.contentType} />
                          </div>

                          <div>
                            <strong>{document.originalFilename}</strong>

                            <span>
                              {getFileExtension(document.originalFilename)}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      {/* Category */}

                      <td>{document.category || "N/A"}</td>

                      {/* File size */}

                      <td>{formatFileSize(document.fileSizeBytes)}</td>

                      {/* Checksum */}

                      <td>
                        <span
                          className="checksum-value"
                          title={document.sha256Checksum || ""}
                        >
                          {document.sha256Checksum
                            ? `${document.sha256Checksum.slice(0, 12)}...`
                            : "N/A"}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      {/* Status */}

                      <td>
                        <DocumentStatus status={document.status} />
                      </td>

                      {/* Uploaded by */}

                      <td>{document.uploadedBy || "N/A"}</td>

                      {/* Upload date */}

                      <td>{formatDocumentDate(document.createdAt)}</td>

                      {/* Actions */}

                      <td>
                        <div className="document-row-actions">
                          {!deleted && (
                            <>
                              {/* Preview */}

                              <button
                                type="button"
                                title="Preview"
                                aria-label={`Preview ${document.originalFilename}`}
                                disabled={
                                  rowLoading ||
                                  !isPreviewable(
                                    document.contentType,
                                    document.originalFilename,
                                  )
                                }
                                onClick={() => handlePreview(document)}
                              >
                                <Eye size={16} />
                              </button>

                              {/* Download */}

                              <button
                                type="button"
                                title="Download"
                                aria-label={`Download ${document.originalFilename}`}
                                disabled={rowLoading}
                                onClick={() => handleDownload(document)}
                              >
                                <Download size={16} />
                              </button>

                              {/* Delete */}

                              <button
                                type="button"
                                title="Delete"
                                aria-label={`Delete ${document.originalFilename}`}
                                className="delete-action"
                                disabled={rowLoading}
                                onClick={() => handleDelete(document)}
                              >
                                <Trash2 size={16} />
                              </button>
                            </>
                          )}

                          {/* Restore */}

                          {deleted && (
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              title="Restore"
                              aria-label={`Restore ${document.originalFilename}`}
                              className="restore-action"
                              disabled={rowLoading}
                              onClick={() => handleRestore(document)}
                            >
                              <RotateCcw size={16} />
                            </button>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Pagination */}

        <div className="documents-pagination">
          <span>
            Page {totalPages > 0 ? page + 1 : 0} of {totalPages}
          </span>

          <div>
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={page <= 0 || loading}
              onClick={() => setPage((current) => current - 1)}
            >
              <ChevronLeft size={17} />
              Previous
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={loading || totalPages === 0 || page >= totalPages - 1}
              onClick={() => setPage((current) => current + 1)}
            >
              Next
              <ChevronRight size={17} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Upload modal */}

      {showUpload && (
        <UploadModal
          onClose={() => setShowUpload(false)}
          onUpload={handleUpload}
          uploading={uploading}
        />
      )}

      {/* Preview modal */}

      {selectedPreviewDocument && (
        <PreviewModal
          document={selectedPreviewDocument}
          previewUrl={previewUrl}
          previewText={previewText}
          onClose={closePreview}
        />
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: pages\Login.jsx

import { useState } from "react";

import {
  ShieldCheck,
  User,
  Lock,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  ArrowRight,
  AlertCircle,
} from "lucide-react";

import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";

import "../styles/login.css";

function Login() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();

  const { login, loading } = useAuth();

  const [credentials, setCredentials] = useState({
    username: "",
    password: "",
  });

  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const handleChange = (event) => {
    const { name, value } = event.target;

    setCredentials((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      [name]: value,
    }));
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");

    if (!credentials.username.trim() || !credentials.password) {
      setError("Please enter both username and password.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      await login(credentials);

      const destination = location.state?.from?.pathname || "/dashboard";

      navigate(destination, { replace: true });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Login failed. Please try again.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="login-page">
      <div className="login-decoration"></div>

      <div className="login-container">
        <div className="login-brand-icon">
          <ShieldCheck size={38} />
        </div>

        <div className="login-heading">
          <h1>Welcome Back</h1>

          <p>Sign in to access your secure evidence vault.</p>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="login-error">
            <AlertCircle size={17} />
            <span>{error}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <div className="form-group">
            <label htmlFor="username">Username</label>

            <div className="input-wrapper">
              <User size={18} />

              <input
                id="username"
                name="username"
                type="text"
                placeholder="Enter your username"
                value={credentials.username}
                onChange={handleChange}
                autoComplete="username"
                required
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="form-group">
            <label htmlFor="password">Password</label>

            <div className="input-wrapper">
              <Lock size={18} />

              <input
                id="password"
                name="password"
                type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                placeholder="Enter your password"
                value={credentials.password}
                onChange={handleChange}
                autoComplete="current-password"
                required
              />

              <button
                type="button"
                className="password-toggle"
                onClick={() => setShowPassword((previous) => !previous)}
                aria-label={showPassword ? "Hide password" : "Show password"}
              >
                {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={18} /> : <Eye size={18} />}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button type="submit" className="login-submit" disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? "Signing In..." : "Sign In"}

            {!loading && <ArrowRight size={18} />}
          </button>
        </form>

        <div className="login-security">
          <ShieldCheck size={15} />

          <span>Secure authentication powered by JWT</span>
        </div>

        <Link to="/" className="back-home">
          Back to Home
        </Link>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default Login;

========================================
FILE: pages\Recovery.jsx
========================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  Activity,
  AlertCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
  Cloud,
  Database,
  FileText,
  RefreshCw,
  ShieldCheck,
  XCircle,
  Zap,
} from "lucide-react";

import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { USER_ROLES } from "../config/constants";

import { getDocuments } from "../services/documentService";
import {
  getDrillHistory,
  getRecoveryManifests,
} from "../services/dashboardService";

import {
  verifyDocument,
  runRecoveryDrill,
  simulatePrimaryFailure,
  simulateCorruption,
} from "../services/recoveryService";

import "../styles/recovery.css";

function Recovery() {
  const { hasRole } = useAuth();
  const isAdmin = hasRole([USER_ROLES.ADMIN]);

  const [documents, setDocuments] = useState([]);
  const [selectedDocumentId, setSelectedDocumentId] = useState("");

  const [history, setHistory] = useState([]);
  const [manifests, setManifests] = useState([]);

  const [verification, setVerification] = useState(null);
  const [operationResult, setOperationResult] = useState(null);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [working, setWorking] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const loadInitialData = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const documentResponse = await getDocuments({
        page: 0,
        size: 100,
      });

      const records = documentResponse?.content || [];
      setDocuments(records);

      if (records.length > 0) {
        setSelectedDocumentId((current) => current || String(records[0].id));
      }

      // Only Admin can access these read-only endpoints.
      if (isAdmin) {
        const results = await Promise.allSettled([
          getDrillHistory(),
          getRecoveryManifests(),
        ]);

        setHistory(
          results[0].status === "fulfilled" && Array.isArray(results[0].value)
            ? results[0].value
            : [],
        );

        setManifests(
          results[1].status === "fulfilled" && Array.isArray(results[1].value)
            ? results[1].value
            : [],
        );
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Unable to load recovery data.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [isAdmin]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadInitialData();
  }, [loadInitialData]);

  const refreshHistory = async () => {
    if (!isAdmin) return;

    const results = await Promise.allSettled([
      getDrillHistory(),
      getRecoveryManifests(),
    ]);

    if (results[0].status === "fulfilled") {
      setHistory(Array.isArray(results[0].value) ? results[0].value : []);
    }

    if (results[1].status === "fulfilled") {
      setManifests(Array.isArray(results[1].value) ? results[1].value : []);
    }
  };

  const executeOperation = async (operation, confirmation) => {
    if (!selectedDocumentId || working) return;

    const confirmed = window.confirm(confirmation);

    if (!confirmed) return;

    setWorking(true);
    setError("");
    setOperationResult(null);

    try {
      const result = await operation(Number(selectedDocumentId));

      setOperationResult(result);

      // Refresh the read-only verification and history after the action.
      const verified = await verifyDocument(Number(selectedDocumentId));

      setVerification(verified);

      await refreshHistory();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Recovery operation failed.");
    } finally {
      setWorking(false);
    }
  };

  const handleVerify = async () => {
    if (!selectedDocumentId || working) return;

    setWorking(true);
    setError("");
    setOperationResult(null);

    try {
      const result = await verifyDocument(Number(selectedDocumentId));

      setVerification(result);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Document verification failed.");
      setVerification(null);
    } finally {
      setWorking(false);
    }
  };

  const formatDate = (value) => {
    if (!value) return "â€”";

    const date = new Date(value);

    return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? value : date.toLocaleString();
  };

  const formatSeconds = (value) => {
    if (value === null || value === undefined) return "â€”";
    return `${Number(value).toFixed(3)} sec`;
  };

  const selectedDocument = documents.find(
    (document) => String(document.id) === selectedDocumentId,
  );

  const primary = verification?.primary;
  const secondary = verification?.secondary;

    const healthy = value === true;

    return (

        {healthy ? "Verified" : "Not verified"}
      </span>
    );
  };

  const drill = operationResult?.drill || operationResult;

  return (
    <main className="recovery-page">

                    ))}

                </table>
              </div>
            )}
          </section>

          <section className="recovery-history-section">
            <div className="recovery-section-heading">
              <div>
                <h2>Recovery Manifests</h2>
                <p>Recorded evidence of recovery operations.</p>
              </div>

              <span>{manifests.length} records</span>
            </div>

            {manifests.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="recovery-empty">
                No recovery manifests are available.
              </div>
            ) : (

                    <tr>
                      <th>Document</th>
                      <th>Failure Type</th>
                      <th>Source Region</th>
                      <th>Destination Region</th>
                      <th>Verification</th>
                      <th>RTO</th>
                      <th>Created At</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>

                  <tbody>
                    {manifests.map((item, index) => (
                      <tr key={item.id ?? index}>
                        <td>#{item.document_id}</td>
                        <td>{item.failure_type || "â€”"}</td>
                        <td>{item.source_region || "â€”"}</td>
                        <td>{item.destination_region || "â€”"}</td>
                        <td>{item.verification_status || "â€”"}</td>
                        <td>{formatSeconds(item.rto_seconds)}</td>
                        <td>{formatDate(item.created_at)}</td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            )}
          </section>
        </>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

export default Recovery;

import { APP_CONFIG } from "../config/constants";

const API_BASE_URL = APP_CONFIG.API_BASE_URL;

async function readResponseBody(response) {
  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") || "";

  try {
    // Handles application/json and application/problem+json.
    if (contentType.toLowerCase().includes("json")) {
      return await response.json();
    }

    return await response.text();
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function extractMessage(data) {
  if (!data) return "";

  if (typeof data === "string") {
    const trimmed = data.trim();

    // Avoid showing a JSON response as raw text.
    if (trimmed.startsWith("{") || trimmed.startsWith("[")) {
      try {
        return extractMessage(JSON.parse(trimmed));
      } catch {
        return trimmed;
      }
    }

    return trimmed;
  }

  if (Array.isArray(data)) {
    return data
      .map((item) => {
        if (typeof item === "string") return item;
        return item?.defaultMessage || item?.message || "";
      })
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(", ");
  }

  if (typeof data === "object") {
    const detail = data.detail;

    if (typeof detail === "string") return detail;

    if (Array.isArray(detail)) {
      return extractMessage(detail);
    }

    return (
      data.message ||
      data.error ||
      data.title ||
      ""
    );
  }

  return "";
}

export function createApiErrorMessage(
  response,
  responseData,
  endpoint = "",
  customFallback = ""
) {
  const status = response.status;

  if (status === 401) {
    if (endpoint.includes("/auth/login")) {
      return "Invalid username or password. Please check your credentials and try again.";
    }

    return "Your session has expired. Please sign in again.";
  }

  if (status === 403) {
    return "You do not have permission to perform this action.";
  }

  if (status === 404) {
    return "The requested resource could not be found.";
  }

  if (status === 409) {
    return "This operation cannot be completed because the resource is in its current state.";
  }

  if (status === 413) {
    return "The selected file is too large to upload.";
  }

  if (status === 415) {
    return "This file format is not supported by the server.";
  }

  if (status === 422) {
    return (
      extractMessage(responseData) ||
      "Some of the submitted information is invalid. Please check your inputs."
    );
  }

  if (status >= 500) {
    return (
      customFallback ||
      "The server encountered an error. Please try again later."
    );
  }

  return (
    extractMessage(responseData) ||
    customFallback ||
    "The request could not be completed. Please try again."
  );
}

export async function getApiErrorMessage(
  response,
  fallback = "",
  endpoint = ""
) {
  const responseData = await readResponseBody(response);

  return createApiErrorMessage(
    response,
    responseData,
    endpoint,
    fallback
  );
}

export async function apiRequest(endpoint, options = {}) {
  const token = localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-token");

  const headers = {
    ...options.headers,
  };

  const isFormData = options.body instanceof FormData;

  // JSON requests need Content-Type.
  // FormData must NOT have a manually assigned Content-Type:
  // the browser adds the multipart boundary automatically.
  if (
    options.body !== undefined &&
    !isFormData &&
    !headers["Content-Type"]
  ) {
    headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json";
  }

  // Do not attach an old JWT to the login request.
  if (token && !endpoint.includes("/auth/login")) {
    headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
  }

  let response;

  try {
    response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${endpoint}`, {
      ...options,
      headers,
    });
  } catch {
    throw new Error(
      "Unable to connect to the server. Check that the backend is running and try again."
    );
  }

  if (response.status === 204) {
    return null;
  }

  const responseData = await readResponseBody(response);

  if (!response.ok) {
    const message = createApiErrorMessage(
      response,
      responseData,
      endpoint
    );

    throw new Error(message);
  }

  return responseData;
}

import { apiRequest } from "./api";

export async function loginUser(credentials) {
  const response = await apiRequest("/v1/auth/login", {
    method: "POST",
    body: JSON.stringify({
      username: credentials.username,
      password: credentials.password,
    }),
  });

  return response;
}

export function saveAuthSession(authData) {
  localStorage.setItem("evidence-vault-token", authData.accessToken);

  localStorage.setItem(
    "evidence-vault-user",
    JSON.stringify({
      username: authData.username,
      roles: authData.roles,
      tokenType: authData.tokenType,
      expiresIn: authData.expiresIn,
    }),
  );
}

export function getStoredUser() {
  const storedUser = localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-user");

  if (!storedUser) {
    return null;
  }

  try {
    return JSON.parse(storedUser);
  } catch {
    clearAuthSession();
    return null;
  }
}

export function clearAuthSession() {
  localStorage.removeItem("evidence-vault-token");
  localStorage.removeItem("evidence-vault-user");
}

import { apiRequest } from "./api";

const DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT = "/v1/documents";

async function getDocumentPage({ status = "", size = 8 } = {}) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({
    page: "0",
    size: String(size),
  });

  if (status) {
    params.set("status", status);
  }

  return apiRequest(`${DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT}?${params.toString()}`);
}

export async function getSystemStatus() {
  return apiRequest("/v1/system/status");
}

export async function getRecoveryMetrics() {
  return apiRequest("/v1/dr/metrics");
}

export async function getDrillHistory() {
  return apiRequest("/v1/dr/drills");
}

export async function getRecoveryManifests() {
  return apiRequest("/v1/dr/manifests");
}

function getSettledValue(result, fallback) {
  return result.status === "fulfilled" ? result.value : fallback;
}

function getSettledError(result) {
  return result.status === "rejected"
    ? result.reason?.message || "Unable to load this information."
    : null;
}

export async function getDashboardData({ includeRecoveryData = false } = {}) {
  const requests = await Promise.allSettled([
    getSystemStatus(),
    getDocumentPage({ size: 8 }),
    getDocumentPage({ status: "ACTIVE", size: 1 }),
    getDocumentPage({ status: "DELETED", size: 1 }),
    getDocumentPage({ status: "CORRUPTED", size: 1 }),

    includeRecoveryData ? getRecoveryMetrics() : Promise.resolve([]),
    includeRecoveryData ? getDrillHistory() : Promise.resolve([]),
    includeRecoveryData ? getRecoveryManifests() : Promise.resolve([]),
  ]);

  const [
    systemResult,
    recentDocumentsResult,
    activeDocumentsResult,
    deletedDocumentsResult,
    corruptedDocumentsResult,
    metricsResult,
    drillsResult,
    manifestsResult,
  ] = requests;

  const recentPage = getSettledValue(recentDocumentsResult, {});
  const activePage = getSettledValue(activeDocumentsResult, {});
  const deletedPage = getSettledValue(deletedDocumentsResult, {});
  const corruptedPage = getSettledValue(corruptedDocumentsResult, {});

  return {
    system: getSettledValue(systemResult, null),

    documents: {
      total: recentPage.totalElements ?? 0,
      active: activePage.totalElements ?? 0,
      deleted: deletedPage.totalElements ?? 0,
      corrupted: corruptedPage.totalElements ?? 0,

      recent: Array.isArray(recentPage.content) ? recentPage.content : [],
    },

    metrics: getSettledValue(metricsResult, []),
    drills: getSettledValue(drillsResult, []),
    manifests: getSettledValue(manifestsResult, []),

    errors: {
      system: getSettledError(systemResult),
      documents: getSettledError(recentDocumentsResult),
      activeDocuments: getSettledError(activeDocumentsResult),
      deletedDocuments: getSettledError(deletedDocumentsResult),
      corruptedDocuments: getSettledError(corruptedDocumentsResult),
      metrics: getSettledError(metricsResult),
      drills: getSettledError(drillsResult),
      manifests: getSettledError(manifestsResult),
    },

    recoveryDataEnabled: includeRecoveryData,
  };
}

export function normalizeArray(data) {
  return Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
}
export function average(values) {
  const valid = values
    .filter((value) => value !== null && value !== undefined && value !== "")
    .map(Number)
    .filter(Number.isFinite);

  if (!valid.length) return null;

  return Number(
    (valid.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / valid.length).toFixed(3),
  );
}

export function calculateDrillSummary(drills) {
  const total = drills.length;

  const successful = drills.filter(
    (item) => item.drill_status === "SUCCESS",
  ).length;

  const failed = drills.filter((item) => item.drill_status === "FAILED").length;

  const attentionRequired = drills.filter(
    (item) => item.drill_status === "RECOVERY_REQUIRED",
  ).length;

  const recoveryOperations = drills.filter(
    (item) => item.recovery_performed === true,
  ).length;

  const corruptionDrills = drills.filter(
    (item) =>
      item.corruption_detected === true ||
      item.failure_type === "SIMULATED_CORRUPTION",
  ).length;

  const primaryFailures = drills.filter(
    (item) => item.failure_type === "SIMULATED_PRIMARY_FAILURE",
  ).length;

  const checksumMismatches = drills.filter((item) => {
    const primaryMismatch =
      item.primary_checksum &&
      item.expected_checksum &&
      item.primary_checksum !== item.expected_checksum;

    const secondaryMismatch =
      item.secondary_checksum &&
      item.expected_checksum &&
      item.secondary_checksum !== item.expected_checksum;

    return primaryMismatch || secondaryMismatch;
  }).length;

  return {
    total,
    successful,
    failed,
    attentionRequired,
    recoveryOperations,
    corruptionDrills,
    primaryFailures,
    checksumMismatches,

    successRate: total ? Number(((successful / total) * 100).toFixed(1)) : null,
  };
}

export function calculateMetricSummary(metrics) {
  const rtoValues = metrics.map(
    (item) => item.recovery_rto_seconds ?? item.recovery_workflow_seconds,
  );

  const rpoValues = metrics
    .map((item) => item.estimated_rpo_seconds)
    .filter((value) => value !== null && value !== undefined);

  const replicationCompleted = metrics.filter(
    (item) => item.replication_status === "COMPLETED",
  ).length;

  const replicationFailed = metrics.filter(
    (item) => item.replication_status === "FAILED",
  ).length;

  const replicationTimedOut = metrics.filter(
    (item) => item.replication_timed_out === true,
  ).length;

  return {
    averageRto: average(rtoValues),
    averageRpo: average(rpoValues),
    replicationCompleted,
    replicationFailed,
    replicationTimedOut,
  };
}

export function getLatestRecord(records) {
  if (!records.length) return null;

  return [...records].sort(
    (a, b) => new Date(b.created_at) - new Date(a.created_at),
  )[0];
}

export function formatSeconds(value) {
  if (
    value === null ||
    value === undefined ||
    !Number.isFinite(Number(value))
  ) {
    return "N/A";
  }

  return `${Number(value).toFixed(3)} sec`;
}

export function formatPercentage(value) {
  return value === null || value === undefined ? "N/A" : `${value}%`;
}

export function formatDate(value) {
  if (!value) return "N/A";

  const date = new Date(value);

  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? "N/A" : date.toLocaleString();
}

FILE: services\documentFileService.js

import { APP_CONFIG } from "../config/constants";

const API_BASE_URL = APP_CONFIG.API_BASE_URL;

function getFriendlyError(status) {
  switch (status) {
    case 401:
      return "Your session has expired. Please sign in again.";
    case 403:
      return "You don't have permission to access this document.";
    case 404:
      return "The document could not be found.";
    case 500:
      return "The server couldn't retrieve this document. Please try again.";
    default:
      return "Unable to retrieve the document. Please try again.";
  }
}

export async function fetchDocumentBlob(documentId) {
  const token = localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-token");

  if (!token) {
    throw new Error("Your session has expired. Please sign in again.");
  }

  const response = await fetch(
    `${API_BASE_URL}/v1/documents/${documentId}/download`,
    {
      method: "GET",
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      },
    },
  );

  if (!response.ok) {
    let message = getFriendlyError(response.status);

    try {
      const errorData = await response.json();

      // Don't expose raw server exceptions to the UI.
      if (response.status >= 500) {
        message =
          "The server couldn't retrieve this document. Please try again.";
      } else if (response.status === 401 || response.status === 403) {
        message = getFriendlyError(response.status);
      } else if (errorData.message) {
        message = errorData.message;
      }
    } catch {
      // Keep the friendly fallback message.
    }

    throw new Error(message);
  }

  return await response.blob();
}

export function downloadBlob(blob, filename) {
  const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

  const link = document.createElement("a");
  link.href = objectUrl;
  link.download = filename || "document";
  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  link.remove();

  URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl);
}

========================================
FILE: services\documentService.js
========================================
import { apiRequest, getApiErrorMessage } from "./api";

import { APP_CONFIG } from "../config/constants";

const DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT = "/v1/documents";
const API_BASE_URL = APP_CONFIG.API_BASE_URL;

function getAuthToken() {
  const token = localStorage.getItem("evidence-vault-token");

  if (!token) {
    throw new Error("Your session has expired. Please sign in again.");
  }

  return token;
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// GET DOCUMENTS
// --------------------------------------------------

export async function getDocuments({
  keyword = "",
  category = "",
  status = "",
  page = 0,
  size = APP_CONFIG.DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE,
} = {}) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();

  params.set("page", String(page));
  params.set("size", String(size));

  if (keyword.trim()) {
    params.set("keyword", keyword.trim());
  }

  if (category.trim()) {
    params.set("category", category.trim());
  }

  if (status.trim()) {
    params.set("status", status.trim());
  }

  return apiRequest(`${DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT}?${params.toString()}`);
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// UPLOAD DOCUMENT
// --------------------------------------------------

export async function uploadDocument(file, category, description = "") {
  if (!(file instanceof File)) {
    throw new Error("Please select a valid document.");
  }

  if (!category?.trim()) {
    throw new Error("Please provide a document category.");
  }

  const formData = new FormData();

  // These names must match the Spring Boot upload endpoint.
  formData.append("file", file);
  formData.append("category", category.trim());

  if (description?.trim()) {
    formData.append("description", description.trim());
  }

  return apiRequest(`${DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT}/upload`, {
    method: "POST",
    body: formData,
  });
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// DELETE DOCUMENT
// --------------------------------------------------

export async function deleteDocument(documentId) {
  if (!documentId) {
    throw new Error("A valid document ID is required.");
  }

  return apiRequest(`${DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT}/${encodeURIComponent(documentId)}`, {
    method: "DELETE",
  });
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// RESTORE DOCUMENT
// --------------------------------------------------

export async function restoreDocument(documentId) {
  if (!documentId) {
    throw new Error("A valid document ID is required.");
  }

  return apiRequest(
    `${DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT}/${encodeURIComponent(documentId)}/restore`,
    {
      method: "POST",
    },
  );
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// COMMON PREVIEW / DOWNLOAD REQUEST
// --------------------------------------------------

async function fetchDocumentFile(documentId, action) {
  if (!documentId) {
    throw new Error("A valid document ID is required.");
  }

  if (!["preview", "download"].includes(action)) {
    throw new Error("Unsupported document operation.");
  }

  const token = getAuthToken();

  const endpoint =
    `${DOCUMENTS_ENDPOINT}/` + `${encodeURIComponent(documentId)}/${action}`;

  let response;

  try {
    response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${endpoint}`, {
      method: "GET",
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        Accept: "*/*",
      },
      cache: "no-store",
    });
  } catch {
    throw new Error(
      `Unable to connect to the server to ${action} this document.`,
    );
  }

  if (!response.ok) {
    const fallback =
      action === "preview"
        ? "The server couldn't preview this document. Please try again later."
        : "The server couldn't download this document. Please try again later.";

    const message = await getApiErrorMessage(response, fallback, endpoint);

    throw new Error(message);
  }

  return response.blob();
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// PREVIEW DOCUMENT
// --------------------------------------------------

export function previewDocument(documentId) {
  return fetchDocumentFile(documentId, "preview");
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// DOWNLOAD DOCUMENT
// --------------------------------------------------

export function downloadDocument(documentId) {
  return fetchDocumentFile(documentId, "download");
}

========================================
FILE: services\documentUtils.js
========================================
export function formatFileSize(bytes) {
  if (bytes === null || bytes === undefined) {
    return "N/A";
  }

  const size = Number(bytes);

  if (!Number.isFinite(size) || size < 0) {
    return "N/A";
  }

  if (size === 0) {
    return "0 Bytes";
  }

  const units = ["Bytes", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"];

  const index = Math.min(
    Math.floor(Math.log(size) / Math.log(1024)),
    units.length - 1,
  );

  return `${(size / Math.pow(1024, index)).toFixed(2)} ${units[index]}`;
}

export function formatDocumentDate(value) {
  if (!value) {
    return "N/A";
  }

  const date = new Date(value);

  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? "N/A" : date.toLocaleString();
}

export function getFileExtension(filename = "") {
  if (!filename || !filename.includes(".")) {
    return "FILE";
  }

  return filename.split(".").pop().toUpperCase();
}

export function getNormalizedContentType(contentType = "") {
  return String(contentType).toLowerCase().split(";")[0].trim();
}

export function isTextDocument(contentType = "", filename = "") {
  const type = getNormalizedContentType(contentType);

  const extension = String(filename).toLowerCase().split(".").pop();

  const textExtensions = ["txt", "csv", "json", "xml", "log", "md"];

  return (
    type.startsWith("text/") ||
    ["application/json", "application/xml"].includes(type) ||
    textExtensions.includes(extension)
  );
}

export function isPreviewable(contentType = "", filename = "") {
  const type = getNormalizedContentType(contentType);

  const extension = String(filename).toLowerCase().split(".").pop();

  const supportedTypes = [
    "application/pdf",
    "image/png",
    "image/jpeg",
    "image/webp",
    "image/gif",
  ];

  const supportedExtensions = [
    "pdf",
    "png",
    "jpg",
    "jpeg",
    "webp",
    "gif",
    "txt",
    "csv",
    "json",
    "xml",
    "log",
    "md",
  ];

  return (
    supportedTypes.includes(type) ||
    type.startsWith("image/") ||
    isTextDocument(type, filename) ||
    supportedExtensions.includes(extension)
  );
}

export function getDocumentStatus(status) {
  return String(status || "UNKNOWN").toUpperCase();
}

    --danger: #ef4444;
}
